Current EG-1's training mixture explains the observed gaps\n\n- Base: `Qwen3-4B-Instruct-2507`.\n- Tune: rank-16 QLoRA, two epochs, 5,656 mostly English examples.\n- Only 259 training outputs are lists.\n- Only three training outputs are two-item lists.\n- The earlier multilingual smoke added only 40 synthetic, non-native-reviewed rows per non-English language. All 160 non-English rows were 2.74% of that mixture.\n\nDecision: reliable short-list and multilingual behavior cannot be inferred from the current data dose.\n\n### 3. Prompt-only list tuning is rejected\n\nExact Mac shipping-path A/B on 75 new positive-list plus 75 restraint cases:\n\n| Metric | Shipping prompt | List prompt |\n|---|---:|---:|\n| List activation | 52/75 | 68/75 |\n| Strict positive success | 11/75 | 15/75 |\n| False lists | 25/75 | 32/75 |\n| Positive scope loss | 15/75 | 23/75 |\n| Meaning-damaging outputs | 8/150 | 12/150 |\n\n- Paired strict gain was four cases, exact McNemar `p=0.125`.\n- The sealed gate required at least eight net strict gains and `p<0.05`.\n- Six meaning-damaging cases occurred only under the candidate prompt.\n\nDecision: do not retune or ship the list-v2 prompt. Prompt changes may remain an experiment arm, but targeted training data is required.\n\n### 4. Several base-model lanes are closed\n\n| Model | Technical result | Decision |\n|---|---|---|\n| Qwen3.5-9B | 40/92 strict, 15 damaging, 7/16 Russian, 2/20 two-item, 0/100 broad list activation | Reject |\n| Phi-4-mini | 18/56 multilingual; 26/92 damaging, including translations and identifier corruption | Reject |\n| Ministral 3 3B | 30/92 strict, 24/56 multilingual, 5/16 Russian, 1/20 two-item | Reject |\n| EuroLLM-9B | Internal wrappers leaked in 2/5 smoke outputs | Reject at smoke |\n\nDecision: do not spend more training or Mac-runtime time on these exact model lanes without new contrary evidence.\n\n### 5. Adapter storage and runtime are viable as a fallback\n\n- Current PEFT adapter: 126.1 MiB.\n- Converted F16 LoRA GGUF: 63.0 MiB.\n- Merged Q5 model: 2.69 GiB.\n- One base plus five current-size F16 adapters: about 3.00 GiB.\n- Five full models: about 13.45 GiB.\n- One selected adapter loaded cleanly with about 79 MiB additional idle memory and sub-second readiness.\n- Simultaneously preloading two adapters failed output isolation.\n\nDecision: if a universal tune fails, the technically viable fallback is one shared base plus exactly one selected adapter loaded at server start, with a local restart when switching. Do not preload multiple active adapters.\n\n### 6. The exact Mac and evaluation safety gates work\n\n- Final integrated suite: 587 passed, 2 expected private-data skips, 118 subtests in 126.54 seconds.\n- Exact private Mac/MPS fail-closed lifecycle: 1,061.131 seconds, exit 0.\n- Production evidence correctly stopped as noncertifying; synthetic evidence correctly stopped as not quality evidence; neither published an evaluation bundle.\n- The fresh 75+75 prompt pilot recomputed 5,028,900 leakage comparisons with no exact or high-similarity violations.\n\nDecision: preserve the exact request path, artifact identity, leakage checks, failure handling, paired scoring, and immutable receipts when simplifying the branch.\n\n## USE WITH CAVEATS\n\nThese are useful leads or tools, not final conclusions.\n\n### Gemma 4 E4B\n\n- Lowest observed meaning damage in the small blind multilingual audit: 1/56.\n- Current EG-1 was 8/56; Qwen3.5-4B was 5/56.\n- Two trained Gemma recipes were rejected because they introduced scope or medical-timing damage.\n- Gemma GGUF experiments were much larger: approximately 5.37-7.48 GiB depending on quantization.\n\nUse: primary serious balanced-data experiment.\n\nCaveat: call it the discovery leader, never a winner. Small development samples do not establish release quality.\n\n### Qwen3.5-4B\n\n- Small development signal was 5/56 damaging outputs with useful German/grammar behavior but weak lists.\n- AlienSV compatibility was proven: 248/248 text modules, all 120 GDN placements, 32,464,896 trainable parameters, one optimizer step in 20.347 seconds, and a 129,927,008-byte adapter.\n\nUse: reserve universal challenger because it is compact and trainable.\n\nCaveat: the one-step adapter proves compatibility only. It is not a quality candidate.\n\n### Language ordering evidence\n\n- Tiny current-EG-1 diagnostic: German 8/8, French 6/8, Spanish 2/8.\n- Russian moved from 3/16 to 7/16 on the same cases when the prompt changed.\n\nUse: prioritize German after English and keep French, Spanish, and Russian in the first balanced comparison.\n\nCaveat: no statistically valid five-language ranking exists. The observed order is small-sample and prompt-sensitive.\n\n### Type B V2 structure\n\n- The 1,890-slot category/length/tier/trap matrix is preserved.\n- It has 23 replacement reserves, 126 packets of 15, and 1,913 custody records.\n- The blocked registry contains 11,236 source rows, 7,198 families, and 13,733 normalized text hashes.\n\nUse: retain the allocation and contamination registry.\n\nCaveat: the content, public-speech source selection, Type B-specific scorer, and final freeze do not exist.\n\n### Authoring/calibration machinery\n\nThe 800-row human-authoring, four-role custody, leakage, ratings, power, and calibration paths are technically hardened.\n\nUse: salvage its immutable-input, family-isolation, rating, and fail-closed patterns.\n\nCaveat: the founder removed human authors/reviewers as an operational dependency. Do not restart the large human-roster workflow. Adapt the useful controls to licensed public speech.\n\n## DO NOT USE\n\nDo not carry these claims or approaches forward:\n\n- The old 93.7%/94.4% result as held-out accuracy.\n- The old Type B or overflow corpora as sealed release benchmarks.\n- The list-v2 prompt as a production fix.\n- Multiple complete language-specific model downloads.\n- Simultaneous multi-adapter preloading in the current llama-server path.\n- The human four-role authoring workflow as the current benchmark plan.\n- Synthetic or model-judged calibration thresholds as production certification.\n- The Qwen3.5 one-step adapter as a trained candidate.\n- The 40-rows-per-language smoke as evidence that universal multilingual tuning works or fails.\n- Any claim that Gemma has won, German support is proven, or the five languages have a stable quality ranking.\n- Any rejected Gemma checkpoint; both failed meaning/scope safety.\n- Any repeated prompt hand-tuning on the sealed 75+75 cases.\n\n## PUBLIC HUMAN-SPEECH BENCHMARK DECISION\n\nBecause native human authors/reviewers are unavailable, use licensed public speech as the next-best reproducible proxy.\n\n### Common five-language benchmark\n\n- Backbone: FLEURS plus MINDS-14.\n- Languages: EN, DE, FR, ES, RU.\n- FLEURS provides broad topics and reference transcripts but is read speech.\n- MINDS-14 provides genuinely spoken queries but is banking-only and has no public speaker IDs.\n- Run public audio through EnviousWispr's real ASR. The ASR output is the polish input; the public transcript anchors content truth.\n\n### English Type B replacement\n\n- Recommended source split: 630 held-out FLEURS units, 630 VoxPopuli units, 630 AMI IHM meeting-speech units.\n- Preserve the existing 1,890-case Type B matrix.\n- List cases may combine 2-5 held-out speech fragments under predeclared list-cue templates, while keeping every component source family attached for leakage control.\n\n### Required caveat\n\nWe can prove separation from Envious-held training and evaluation data. We cannot prove that public transcripts were absent from the unknown pretraining corpus of a base model such as Qwen. State this limitation in every release claim.\n\n## MISSING WORK\n\nThe following deliverables do not exist yet:\n\n1. Final pinned source/revision/license contract for the web-speech datasets.\n2. Tested metadata verifier and ignored audio downloader.\n3. Materialized 800-row multilingual development proxy.\n4. Sealed minimum 1,600-row multilingual frozen proxy.\n5. Public-speech content and dedicated scoring for Type B V2.\n6. Comparable vanilla Qwen3, current EG-1, prompt, Gemma, and Qwen3.5 runs on one common development set.\n7. A serious balanced training-only corpus.\n8. A promoted model candidate.\n9. One-time frozen, Type B V2, and exact shipped-Mac finalist results.\n10. Production packaging or language-selection UI.\n\n## SHORTEST SALVAGE PATH\n\nDo this in order. Do not add more research lanes until this produces comparable model numbers.\n\n1. Finalize FLEURS + MINDS-14 source revisions, licenses, selection rules, family keys, and source hashes.\n2. Materialize development only: 160 cases per language, 800 total. Keep frozen selections committed only by private seed/hash and do not generate candidate outputs for them.\n3. Run the 800 public audio cases through the real EnviousWispr ASR path.\n4. Compare, on identical inputs and scoring, only these initial arms: vanilla Qwen3, shipping EG-1, the already-rejected prompt as a control, untouched Gemma, and untouched Qwen3.5-4B.\n5. Use the result to create one balanced training-only dose covering language, list length, restraint, corrections, identifiers, numbers, medical timing, and no-op preservation.\n6. Train exactly two candidates in parallel on AlienSV: Gemma primary and Qwen3.5-4B reserve.\n7. For each recipe, run one smoke and one full development benchmark. Stop any recipe that does not improve the target without meaningful safety regressions.\n8. Lock one finalist. Only then materialize/open frozen outputs and run the new 1,890-case Type B once.\n9. Run the survivor through the quantized exact shipped Mac path.\n10. Only after a benchmark win, design packaging or adapter-selection UX.\n\n## STOP RULES\n\n- One smoke plus one full development run per exact recipe.\n- No repeated tuning against frozen, Type B, or the sealed 75+75 prompt pilot.\n- No aggregate headline may hide a failing language or safety slice.\n- Meaning, scope, names, identifiers, numbers, negation, corrections, and timing are hard safety gates.\n- If neither serious universal tune wins, then test one shared-base-plus-selected-adapter fallback. Do not jump to multiple full models.\n\n## DEFINITION OF THE NEXT MEANINGFUL RESULT\n\nThe next update should not be another infrastructure count. It should be one comparison table showing the same 800 public-speech development cases run through the five initial arms, with per-language list success, restraint/false-list rate, meaning/scope damage, inference failures, paired uncertainty, exact prompt/artifact identity, and raw-output receipts.\n\nThat table is the decision point for whether to train Gemma, Qwen3.5-4B, both, or neither.\n\n## FILE MAP\n\n| Need | File |\n|---|---|\n| Concise full-run index | `WORK-INDEX-2026-07-15.md` |\n| Founder-readable summary | `FOUNDER-READOUT-2026-07-15.md` |\n| Every chronological experiment receipt | `OVERNIGHT-LOG-2026-07-15.md` |\n| Web-speech benchmark design | `WEB-SPEECH-PROXY-BENCHMARK-V1.md` |\n| Five-language benchmark contract | `MULTILINGUAL-BENCHMARK-V2-SPEC.md` |\n| Training data design | `TRAINING-DATA-DESIGN-V2.md` |\n| Model comparison | `MODEL-SCORECARD-V1.md` |\n| Qwen language evidence | `QWEN3-LANGUAGE-EVIDENCE-AUDIT-2026-07-15.md` |\n| Qwen3.5 LoRA compatibility | `QWEN35-RESERVE-BF16-LORA-PREFLIGHT-2026-07-15.md` |\n\n## BRANCH HANDLING\n\nPR #1562 is too broad to treat as a product PR.      1	#!/usr/bin/env python3
     2	"""Generate and validate model-blind English list benchmark candidates.
     3	
     4	The generator builds two separate development corpora:
     5	
     6	* positive list cases that should become bullets or numbered lines;
     7	* prose-restraint cases whose enumerations must remain prose.
     8	
     9	Rows are model-assisted candidate gold, never frozen or training data. The
    10	script screens only corpus inputs. It never reads candidate model outputs.
    11	"""
    12	
    13	from __future__ import annotations
    14	
    15	import argparse
    16	import hashlib
    17	import json
    18	import os
    19	import random
    20	import re
    21	import subprocess
    22	import tempfile
    23	import time
    24	import unicodedata
    25	from collections import Counter
    26	from dataclasses import asdict, dataclass
    27	from difflib import SequenceMatcher
    28	from itertools import product
    29	from pathlib import Path
    30	from typing import Any, Callable
    31	
    32	
    33	DOMAINS = ("work_admin", "personal_home", "technical_product", "medical", "legal_financial")
    34	POSITIVE_TYPES = ("explicit_bullets", "scoped_deliverables", "natural_enumeration", "spoken_ordinals")
    35	RESTRAINT_TYPES = ("woven_argument", "descriptive_series", "quoted_reported", "conditional_narrative")
    36	ITEM_COUNTS = (2, 3, 4, 5)
    37	LENGTH_BUCKETS = {
    38	    "short": (12, 22),
    39	    "medium": (23, 38),
    40	    "long": (39, 58),
    41	    "extended": (59, 85),
    42	}
    43	INPUT_FIELDS = ("input", "asr_input", "raw", "transcript")
    44	EXPECTED_FIELDS = {
    45	    "spec_id",
    46	    "family",
    47	    "input",
    48	    "expected_output",
    49	    "items",
    50	    "compound_items",
    51	    "scope_anchors",
    52	    "forbidden",
    53	    "audit_note",
    54	}
    55	SIMILARITY_THRESHOLDS = {
    56	    "sequence_ratio": 0.82,
    57	    "token_jaccard": 0.78,
    58	    "char_4gram_jaccard": 0.75,
    59	}

    62	@dataclass(frozen=True)
    63	class CaseSpec:
    64	    spec_id: str
    65	    role: str
    66	    domain: str
    67	    case_type: str
    68	    item_count: int
    69	    length_bucket: str
    70	    compound_required: bool

    76	    source_id: str
    77	    normalized: str
    78	    tokens: frozenset[str]
    79	    char_4grams: frozenset[str]

    96	    parser.add_argument("--prior-batch", action="append", default=[])
    97	    parser.add_argument("--mode", choices=("generate", "validate"), default="generate")
    98	    return parser.parse_args()

   100	
   101	def normalized(value: str) -> str:
   102	    value = unicodedata.normalize("NFKC", value).casefold()
   103	    return re.sub(r"[^\w]+", " ", value, flags=re.UNICODE).strip()
   104	
   105	
   106	def sha256_bytes(value: bytes) -> str:
   107	    return hashlib.sha256(value).hexdigest()
   108	
   109	
   110	def file_sha256(path: Path) -> str:
   111	    digest = hashlib.sha256()
   112	    with path.open("rb") as handle:
   113	        for chunk in iter(lambda: handle.read(1024 * 1024), b""):
   114	            digest.update(chunk)
   115	    return digest.hexdigest()
   116	
   117	
   118	def char_ngrams(value: str, size: int = 4) -> frozenset[str]:
   119	    compact = value.replace(" ", "_")
   120	    if len(compact) < size:
   121	        return frozenset({compact}) if compact else frozenset()
   122	    return frozenset(compact[index : index + size] for index in range(len(compact) - size + 1))
   123	
   124	
   125	def jaccard(left: frozenset[str], right: frozenset[str]) -> float:
   126	    if not left or not right:
   127	        return 0.0
   128	    return len(left & right) / len(left | right)
   129	
   130	
   131	def similarity(left: AuditInput, right: AuditInput) -> tuple[float, dict[str, float]]:
   132	    length_ratio = min(len(left.normalized), len(right.normalized)) / max(
   133	        len(left.normalized), len(right.normalized), 1
   134	    )
   135	    sequence_ratio = 0.0
   136	    if length_ratio >= 0.50:
   137	        sequence_ratio = SequenceMatcher(None, left.normalized, right.normalized, autojunk=False).ratio()
   138	    scores = {
   139	        "sequence_ratio": sequence_ratio,
   140	        "token_jaccard": jaccard(left.tokens, right.tokens),
   141	        "char_4gram_jaccard": jaccard(left.char_4grams, right.char_4grams),
   142	    }
   143	    return max(scores.values()), scores
   144	
   145	
   146	def input_value(row: dict[str, Any]) -> str | None:
   147	    for field in INPUT_FIELDS:
   148	        value = row.get(field)
   149	        if isinstance(value, str) and value.strip():
   150	            return value
   151	    return None
   152	
   153	
   154	def load_json_rows_bytes(path: Path, value: bytes) -> list[dict[str, Any]]:
   155	    rows: list[dict[str, Any]] = []
   156	    try:
   157	        text = value.decode("utf-8")
   158	    except UnicodeDecodeError as error:
   159	        raise ValueError(f"{path} is not UTF-8") from error
   160	    if path.suffix == ".jsonl":
   161	        for line_number, line in enumerate(text.splitlines(), 1):
   162	            if not line.strip():
   163	                continue
   164	            row = json.loads(line)
   165	            if not isinstance(row, dict):
   166	                raise ValueError(f"{path}:{line_number} is not a JSON object")
   167	            rows.append(row)
   168	        return rows
   169	    parsed = json.loads(text)
   170	    if isinstance(parsed, list):
   171	        rows = parsed
   172	    elif isinstance(parsed, dict):
   173	        for key in ("rows", "cases", "data"):
   174	            if isinstance(parsed.get(key), list):
   175	                rows = parsed[key]
   176	                break
   177	    if not rows or not all(isinstance(row, dict) for row in rows):
   178	        raise ValueError(f"{path} has no supported row array")
   179	    return rows
   180	
   181	
   182	def load_json_rows(path: Path) -> list[dict[str, Any]]:
   183	    return load_json_rows_bytes(path, path.read_bytes())
   184	
   185	
   186	def load_audit_sources(paths: list[str]) -> tuple[list[AuditInput], list[dict[str, Any]], set[str]]:
   187	    audit_inputs: list[AuditInput] = []
   188	    inventories: list[dict[str, Any]] = []
   189	    families: set[str] = set()
   190	    for raw_path in paths:
   191	        path = Path(raw_path).resolve()
   192	        if not path.is_file():
   193	            raise ValueError(f"audit source does not exist: {path}")
   194	        source_bytes = path.read_bytes()
   195	        rows = load_json_rows_bytes(path, source_bytes)
   196	        source_sha = sha256_bytes(source_bytes)
   197	        input_count = 0
   198	        for index, row in enumerate(rows, 1):
   199	            family = row.get("family")
   200	            if isinstance(family, str) and family.strip():
   201	                families.add(normalized(family))
   202	            value = input_value(row)
   203	            if value is None:
   204	                continue
   205	            key = normalized(value)
   206	            if not key:
   207	                continue
   208	            input_count += 1
   209	            audit_inputs.append(
   210	                AuditInput(
   211	                    source=str(path),
   212	                    source_id=str(row.get("id", index)),
   213	                    normalized=key,
   214	                    tokens=frozenset(key.split()),
   215	                    char_4grams=char_ngrams(key),
   216	                )
   217	            )
   218	        inventories.append(
   219	            {
   220	                "path": str(path),
   221	                "sha256": source_sha,
   222	                "row_count": len(rows),
   223	                "input_count": input_count,
   224	            }
   225	        )
   226	    if not audit_inputs:
   227	        raise ValueError("audit sources contain no input/asr_input/raw/transcript values")
   228	    return audit_inputs, inventories, families
   229	
   230	
   231	def pairwise_balance(specs: list[CaseSpec]) -> dict[str, dict[str, int]]:
   232	    axes = ("domain", "case_type", "item_count", "length_bucket", "compound_required")
   233	    axis_values = {
   234	        axis: sorted({getattr(spec, axis) for spec in specs}, key=str)
   235	        for axis in axes
   236	    }
   237	    result: dict[str, dict[str, int]] = {}
   238	    for left_index, left in enumerate(axes):
   239	        for right in axes[left_index + 1 :]:
   240	            counts: Counter[str] = Counter(
   241	                f"{getattr(spec, left)}|{getattr(spec, right)}" for spec in specs
   242	            )
   243	            result[f"{left}_x_{right}"] = {
   244	                f"{left_value}|{right_value}": counts[f"{left_value}|{right_value}"]
   245	                for left_value, right_value in product(axis_values[left], axis_values[right])
   246	            }
   247	    return result
   248	
   249	
   250	def balanced_specs(role: str, count: int, seed: int) -> list[CaseSpec]:
   251	    if count <= 0 or count % 20:
   252	        raise ValueError("--count-per-role must be a positive multiple of 20")
   253	    case_types = POSITIVE_TYPES if role == "positive_list" else RESTRAINT_TYPES
   254	    repeats = count // (len(DOMAINS) * len(case_types))
   255	    slots = [
   256	        (domain, case_type, repeat, bool((domain_index + type_index + repeat) % 2))
   257	        for domain_index, domain in enumerate(DOMAINS)
   258	        for type_index, case_type in enumerate(case_types)
   259	        for repeat in range(repeats)
   260	    ]
     1	#!/usr/bin/env python3
     2	"""Score the sealed EG-1 English list A/B with explicit arm binding."""

     7	import hashlib
     8	import json
     9	import os
    10	from pathlib import Path
    11	import re
    12	import tempfile
    13	from typing import Any, Callable
    14	
    15	import score_eg1_english_list_novel as deterministic
    16	from eg1_english_list_contract import (
    17	    load_contract,
    18	    require_binding,
    19	    validate_noncertifying_ab_runtime_receipt,
    20	    validate_binding_commit,
    21	)

    24	SCRIPT_PATH = Path(__file__).resolve()
    25	REPO_ROOT = SCRIPT_PATH.parents[2]
    26	EVAL_DIR = SCRIPT_PATH.parent
    27	CONTRACT_VERIFIER = EVAL_DIR / "eg1_english_list_contract.py"
    28	DETERMINISTIC_SCORER = Path(deterministic.__file__).resolve()
    29	CANONICAL_DECISION_CONTRACT = (
    30	    REPO_ROOT
    31	    / "docs"
    32	    / "experiments"
    33	    / "eg1-multilingual"
    34	    / "ENGLISH-LIST-PILOT75-DECISION-CONTRACT-V2.md"
    35	)
    36	EXPECTED_ARMS = ("baseline", "candidate")

    39	def parse_args() -> argparse.Namespace:
    40	    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser()
    41	    parser.add_argument("--positive-corpus", required=True, type=Path)
    42	    parser.add_argument("--restraint-corpus", required=True, type=Path)
    43	    parser.add_argument("--ab-bundle", required=True, type=Path)
    44	    parser.add_argument("--expected-ab-receipt-sha256", required=True)
    45	    parser.add_argument("--out", required=True, type=Path)
    46	    return parser.parse_args()

    48	
    49	def sha256_bytes(value: bytes) -> str:
    50	    return hashlib.sha256(value).hexdigest()
    51	
    52	
    53	def read_once(path: Path, label: str) -> tuple[bytes, str]:
    54	    try:
    55	        value = path.read_bytes()
    56	    except OSError as error:
    57	        raise ValueError(f"cannot read {label}") from error
    58	    return value, sha256_bytes(value)
    59	
    60	
    61	def parse_json(value: bytes, label: str) -> dict[str, Any]:
    62	    try:
    63	        parsed = json.loads(value)
    64	    except json.JSONDecodeError as error:
    65	        raise ValueError(f"{label} is invalid JSON") from error
    66	    if not isinstance(parsed, dict):
    67	        raise ValueError(f"{label} is not an object")
    68	    return parsed
    69	
    70	
    71	def require_sha(actual: str, expected: Any, label: str) -> None:
    72	    if not isinstance(expected, str) or not re.fullmatch(r"[0-9a-f]{64}", expected):
    73	        raise ValueError(f"expected {label} SHA-256 is invalid")
    74	    if actual != expected:
    75	        raise ValueError(f"{label} differs from its bound SHA-256")
    76	
    77	
    78	def resolve_recorded_path(value: Any, label: str) -> Path:
    79	    if not isinstance(value, str) or not value:
    80	        raise ValueError(f"{label} path is invalid")
    81	    path = Path(value)
    82	    if path.is_absolute():
    83	        return path
    84	    if ".." in path.parts:
    85	        raise ValueError(f"{label} path escapes the repository")
    86	    return REPO_ROOT / path
    87	
    88	
    89	def direct_child(bundle: Path, value: Any, label: str) -> Path:
    90	    if not isinstance(value, str) or not value:
    91	        raise ValueError(f"{label} path is invalid")
    92	    relative = Path(value)
    93	    if relative.is_absolute() or ".." in relative.parts or len(relative.parts) != 1:
    94	        raise ValueError(f"{label} path is not a direct bundle child")
    95	    path = bundle / relative
    96	    if not path.is_file() or path.is_symlink():
    97	        raise ValueError(f"{label} is unavailable")
    98	    return path
    99	
   100	
   101	def publish_exclusive(
   102	    path: Path, value: bytes, before_link: Callable[[], None] | None = None
   103	) -> None:
   104	    fd, temp_name = tempfile.mkstemp(prefix=f".{path.name}.", dir=path.parent)
   105	    temp = Path(temp_name)
   106	    try:
   107	        with os.fdopen(fd, "wb") as handle:
   108	            handle.write(value)
   109	            handle.flush()
   110	            os.fsync(handle.fileno())
   111	        if before_link is not None:
   112	            before_link()
   113	        os.link(temp, path)
   114	    finally:
   115	        temp.unlink(missing_ok=True)
   116	
   117	
   118	def load_bound_inputs(
   119	    positive: Path,
   120	    restraint: Path,
   121	    bundle: Path,
   122	    expected_ab_receipt_sha: str,
   123	) -> tuple[dict[str, Any], Path, Path, Path, str]:
   124	    ab_receipt_path = bundle / "receipt.json"
   125	    ab_bytes, ab_sha = read_once(ab_receipt_path, "A/B receipt")
   126	    require_sha(ab_sha, expected_ab_receipt_sha, "A/B receipt")
   127	    ab = parse_json(ab_bytes, "A/B receipt")
   128	    validate_noncertifying_ab_runtime_receipt(ab)
   129	
   130	    try:
   131	        provenance = ab["provenance"]
   132	        source_records = provenance["sources"]
   133	        contract_record = source_records["decision_contract"]
   134	    except (KeyError, TypeError) as error:
   135	        raise ValueError("A/B receipt does not bind the decision contract") from error
   136	    contract_path = resolve_recorded_path(
   137	        contract_record.get("path"), "decision contract"
   138	    )
   139	    if contract_path.resolve() != CANONICAL_DECISION_CONTRACT.resolve():
   140	        raise ValueError("A/B receipt does not bind the canonical V2 contract")
   141	    contract_bytes, contract_sha = read_once(contract_path, "decision contract")
   142	    require_sha(contract_sha, contract_record.get("sha256"), "decision contract")
   143	    loaded_bytes, loaded_sha, bindings = load_contract(contract_path)
   144	    if loaded_bytes != contract_bytes or loaded_sha != contract_sha:
   145	        raise RuntimeError("decision contract changed while loading")
   146	    if provenance.get("bindings") != bindings:
   147	        raise ValueError("A/B receipt bindings differ from the executable contract")
   148	    for key, path in (
   149	        ("contract_verifier_sha256", CONTRACT_VERIFIER),
   150	        ("deterministic_scorer_sha256", DETERMINISTIC_SCORER),
   151	        ("ab_scorer_sha256", SCRIPT_PATH),
   152	    ):
   153	        _, actual_sha = read_once(path, key)
   154	        require_binding(bindings, key, actual_sha)
   155	    execution_head = validate_binding_commit(
   156	        bindings, contract_path, REPO_ROOT
   157	    )
   158	    if provenance.get("git_head") != execution_head:
   159	        raise ValueError("A/B receipt Git head differs from the contract binding commit")
   160	
   161	    arm_paths: dict[str, Path] = {}
   162	    for arm in EXPECTED_ARMS:
   163	        try:
   164	            record = ab["arms"][arm]
   165	        except (KeyError, TypeError) as error:
   166	            raise ValueError(f"A/B receipt is missing {arm}") from error
   167	        if not isinstance(record, dict):
   168	            raise ValueError(f"A/B receipt {arm} record is invalid")
   169	        if (
   170	            record.get("inference_error_count") != 0
   171	            or record.get("empty_output_count") != 0
   172	            or record.get("runner_returncode") != 0
   173	            or record.get("row_count") != 150
   174	        ):
   175	            raise ValueError(f"A/B receipt {arm} failed inference health")
   176	        path = direct_child(bundle, record.get("path"), f"{arm} output")
   177	        value, actual_sha = read_once(path, f"{arm} output")
   178	        require_sha(actual_sha, record.get("sha256"), f"{arm} output")
   179	        rows = deterministic.read_jsonl(path)
   180	        if any("model_id" in row for row in rows):
   181	            raise ValueError(f"{arm} output may not override receipt-bound arm identity")
   182	        arm_paths[arm] = path
   183	
   184	    try:
   185	        render_record = ab["provenance"]["render_receipt"]
   186	    except (KeyError, TypeError) as error:
   187	        raise ValueError("A/B receipt does not bind the render receipt") from error
   188	    render_path = resolve_recorded_path(render_record.get("path"), "render receipt")
   189	    render_bytes, render_sha = read_once(render_path, "render receipt")
   190	    require_sha(render_sha, render_record.get("sha256"), "render receipt")
   191	    render = parse_json(render_bytes, "render receipt")
   192	    try:
   193	        render_bindings = render["provenance"]["bindings"]
   194	    except (KeyError, TypeError) as error:
   195	        raise ValueError("render receipt does not carry contract bindings") from error
   196	    if render_bindings != bindings:
   197	        raise ValueError("render receipt bindings differ from the executable contract")
   198	    try:
   199	        positive_source = render["sources"]["positive_corpus"]
   200	        restraint_source = render["sources"]["restraint_corpus"]
   201	    except (KeyError, TypeError) as error:
   202	        raise ValueError("render receipt does not bind both corpora") from error
   203	    for supplied, source, label in (
   204	        (positive, positive_source, "positive corpus"),
   205	        (restraint, restraint_source, "restraint corpus"),
   206	    ):
   207	        recorded = resolve_recorded_path(source.get("path"), label)
   208	        if recorded.resolve() != supplied.resolve():
   209	            raise ValueError(f"supplied {label} is not receipt-bound")
   210	        _, actual_sha = read_once(supplied, label)
   211	        require_sha(actual_sha, source.get("sha256"), label)
   212	
   213	    ab["_verified_contract"] = {
   214	        "path": str(contract_path),
   215	        "sha256": contract_sha,
   216	        "bindings": bindings,
   217	        "git_head": execution_head,
   218	    }
   219	    return ab, arm_paths["baseline"], arm_paths["candidate"], render_path, ab_sha
   220	
   221	
   222	def role_losses(model: dict[str, Any], role: str, key: str) -> int:
   223	    return sum(
   224	        row["inference_ok"] and not row[key]
   225	        for row in model["case_results"]
   226	        if row["role"] == role
   227	    )
   228	
   229	
   230	def mechanical_gate(report: dict[str, Any]) -> dict[str, Any]:
   231	    baseline = report["models"]["baseline"]
   232	    candidate = report["models"]["candidate"]
   233	    comparison = next(
   234	        item
   235	        for item in report["paired_comparisons"]
   236	        if item["left_model"] == "baseline" and item["right_model"] == "candidate"
   237	    )
   238	    positive = comparison["positive_strict"]
   239	    net_gain = positive["right_only"] - positive["left_only"]
   240	    baseline_by_id = {row["id"]: row for row in baseline["case_results"]}
   241	    candidate_by_id = {row["id"]: row for row in candidate["case_results"]}
   242	    restraint_ids = [
   243	        case_id
   244	        for case_id, row in baseline_by_id.items()
   245	        if row["role"] == "prose_restraint"
   246	    ]
   247	    candidate_only_false_lists = [
   248	        case_id
   249	        for case_id in restraint_ids
   250	        if candidate_by_id[case_id]["false_list"] and not baseline_by_id[case_id]["false_list"]
   251	    ]
   252	    loss_checks: dict[str, dict[str, Any]] = {}
   253	    for role in ("positive_list", "prose_restraint"):
   254	        for key in ("items_preserved", "scope_preserved"):
   255	            name = f"{role}.{key.replace('_preserved', '_loss')}"
   256	            baseline_loss = role_losses(baseline, role, key)
   257	            candidate_loss = role_losses(candidate, role, key)
   258	            loss_checks[name] = {
   259	                "baseline": baseline_loss,
   260	                "candidate": candidate_loss,
   261	                "pass": candidate_loss <= baseline_loss,
   262	            }
   263	
   264	    conditions = {
   265	        "both_arms_inference_healthy": {
   266	            "pass": baseline["inference_failure_count"] == 0
   267	            and candidate["inference_failure_count"] == 0,
   268	        },
   269	        "positive_net_gain_at_least_8": {"value": net_gain, "pass": net_gain >= 8},
   270	        "positive_mcnemar_p_below_0_05": {
   271	            "value": positive["exact_mcnemar_p_two_sided"],
   272	            "pass": positive["exact_mcnemar_p_two_sided"] < 0.05,
   273	        },
   274	        "zero_candidate_only_restraint_false_lists": {
   275	            "ids": candidate_only_false_lists,
   276	            "pass": not candidate_only_false_lists,
   277	        },
   278	        "candidate_total_false_lists_not_above_baseline": {
   279	            "baseline": baseline["restraint"]["false_list"]["successes"],
   280	            "candidate": candidate["restraint"]["false_list"]["successes"],
   281	            "pass": candidate["restraint"]["false_list"]["successes"]
   282	            <= baseline["restraint"]["false_list"]["successes"],
   283	        },
   284	        "item_and_scope_loss_not_increased_per_lane": {
   285	            "details": loss_checks,
   286	            "pass": all(item["pass"] for item in loss_checks.values()),
   287	        },
   288	    }
   289	    return {
   290	        "mechanical_conditions": conditions,
   291	        "mechanical_pass": all(item["pass"] for item in conditions.values()),
   292	        "semantic_condition": "pending_arm_blind_review",
   293	        "candidate_advances": False,
   294	        "candidate_advances_reason": "semantic review is required after mechanical scoring",
   295	    }
   296	
   297	
   298	def main() -> int:
   299	    args = parse_args()

     1	#!/usr/bin/env python3
     2	"""Reproducible multilingual EG-1 adapter training on AlienSV.
     3	
     4	The historical Gemma/older-Qwen path remains QLoRA. Qwen3.5 deliberately uses
     5	BF16 LoRA because Unsloth warns against Qwen3.5 4-bit training.
     6	"""
     7	
     8	from __future__ import annotations
     9	
    10	import argparse

   100	def parse_args() -> argparse.Namespace:
   101	    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser()
   102	    parser.add_argument("--base", required=True)
   103	    parser.add_argument("--data", required=True)
   104	    parser.add_argument("--prompt", required=True)
   105	    parser.add_argument("--tag", required=True)
   106	    parser.add_argument("--output-root", default="~/tuning/out")
   107	    parser.add_argument("--lr", type=float, default=5e-5)
   108	    parser.add_argument("--epochs", type=float, default=2.0)
   109	    parser.add_argument("--rank", type=int, default=16)
   110	    parser.add_argument("--alpha", type=int, default=32)
   111	    parser.add_argument("--micro-batch", type=int, default=4)
   112	    parser.add_argument("--gradient-accumulation", type=int, default=4)
   113	    parser.add_argument("--max-seq", type=int, default=512)
   114	    parser.add_argument("--seed", type=int, default=1265)
   115	    parser.add_argument(
   116	        "--training-mode",
   117	        choices=("auto", QLORA_MODE, BF16_LORA_MODE),
   118	        default="auto",
   119	        help="auto keeps historical bases on QLoRA and routes Qwen3.5 to BF16 LoRA",
   120	    )
   121	    parser.add_argument(
   122	        "--preflight-only",
   123	        action="store_true",
   124	        help="run exactly one compatibility step; this is never model-quality evidence",
   125	    )
   126	    parser.add_argument("--skip-merge", action="store_true")
   127	    return parser.parse_args()
   128	
   129	
   130	def model_family(config: dict[str, Any]) -> str:
   131	    model_type = str(config.get("model_type", "")).casefold().replace("_", "")
   132	    architectures = " ".join(str(item) for item in config.get("architectures", []))
   133	    if "qwen35" in model_type or "qwen3_5" in architectures.casefold():
   134	        return QWEN35_FAMILY
   135	    if "gemma" in model_type or "gemma" in architectures.casefold():
   136	        return "gemma"
   137	    return "other"
   138	
   139	
   140	def resolve_training_mode(family: str, requested: str) -> str:
   141	    resolved = BF16_LORA_MODE if family == QWEN35_FAMILY else QLORA_MODE
   142	    if requested != "auto":
   143	        resolved = requested
   144	    if family == QWEN35_FAMILY and resolved == QLORA_MODE:
   145	        raise ValueError(
   146	            "Refusing Qwen3.5 QLoRA: Unsloth recommends BF16 LoRA because its "
   147	            "4-bit quantization differences are higher than normal"
   148	        )
   149	    if family != QWEN35_FAMILY and resolved == BF16_LORA_MODE:
   150	        raise ValueError("BF16 LoRA is currently implemented only for Qwen3.5")
   151	    return resolved
   152	
   153	
   154	def validate_preflight_request(
   155	    *,
   156	    family: str,
   157	    enabled: bool,
   158	    rows: list[dict[str, Any]],
   159	    skip_merge: bool,
   160	    data_sha256: str,
   161	    prompt_sha256: str | None,
   162	    rank: int,
   163	    learning_rate: float,
   164	) -> None:
   165	    if family == QWEN35_FAMILY and not enabled:
   166	        raise ValueError(
   167	            "Qwen3.5 full training is not authorized; --preflight-only is currently required"
   168	        )
   169	    if not enabled:
   170	        return
   171	    if family != QWEN35_FAMILY:
   172	        raise ValueError("--preflight-only is currently authorized only for Qwen3.5")
   173	    if len(rows) not in range(2, 5):
   174	        raise ValueError("--preflight-only requires exactly 2 to 4 private synthetic rows")
   175	    if not skip_merge:
   176	        raise ValueError("--preflight-only requires --skip-merge")
   177	    if rank != 16:
   178	        raise ValueError("--preflight-only requires the script-owned rank-16 contract")
   179	    qwen35_preflight_first_step_learning_rate(learning_rate)
   180	    expected_data_sha256 = str(QWEN35_PREFLIGHT_CONTRACT["data_sha256"])
   181	    if data_sha256 != expected_data_sha256:
   182	        raise ValueError(
   183	            f"Preflight data SHA-256 mismatch: expected {expected_data_sha256}, got {data_sha256}"
   184	        )
   185	    expected_prompt_sha256 = str(QWEN35_PREFLIGHT_CONTRACT["prompt_sha256"])
   186	    if prompt_sha256 != expected_prompt_sha256:
   187	        raise ValueError(
   188	            "Preflight prompt SHA-256 mismatch: "
   189	            f"expected {expected_prompt_sha256}, got {prompt_sha256 or 'no prompt hash'}"
   190	        )
   191	    allowed_keys = {"input", "output", "preflight_provenance"}
   192	    for index, row in enumerate(rows):
   193	        if set(row) != allowed_keys:
   194	            raise ValueError(
   195	                f"Preflight row {index} must contain only {sorted(allowed_keys)}; "
   196	                "benchmark/D1 metadata is forbidden"
   197	            )
   198	        if row["preflight_provenance"] != QWEN35_PREFLIGHT_CONTRACT["row_provenance"]:
   199	            raise ValueError(
   200	                f"Preflight row {index} lacks the exact private synthetic provenance marker"
   201	            )
   202	        if not row["input"].strip() or not row["output"].strip():
   203	            raise ValueError(f"Preflight row {index} has an empty input or output")
   204	    inputs = [row["input"].strip().casefold() for row in rows]
   205	    outputs = [row["output"].strip().casefold() for row in rows]
   206	    if len(set(inputs)) != len(inputs) or len(set(outputs)) != len(outputs):
   207	        raise ValueError("Preflight rows must have unique inputs and outputs")
   208	
   209	
   210	def effective_warmup_ratio(family: str, preflight_only: bool) -> float:
   211	    if family == QWEN35_FAMILY and preflight_only:
   212	        return float(QWEN35_PREFLIGHT_CONTRACT["warmup_ratio"])
   213	    return 0.05
   214	
   215	
   216	def qwen35_preflight_first_step_learning_rate(learning_rate: float) -> float:
   217	    max_steps = int(QWEN35_PREFLIGHT_CONTRACT["max_steps"])
   218	    scheduler = str(QWEN35_PREFLIGHT_CONTRACT["scheduler"])
   219	    warmup_ratio = float(QWEN35_PREFLIGHT_CONTRACT["warmup_ratio"])
   220	    warmup_steps = math.ceil(max_steps * warmup_ratio)
   221	    if max_steps != 1 or scheduler != "cosine" or warmup_steps != 0:
   222	        raise ValueError(
   223	            "Qwen3.5 preflight scheduler contract requires one cosine step with zero warmup"
   224	        )
   225	    if not math.isfinite(learning_rate) or learning_rate <= 0:
   226	        raise ValueError("Qwen3.5 preflight learning rate must be finite and positive")
   227	    return learning_rate
   228	
   229	
   230	def qwen35_training_stack_receipt(
   231	    package_versions: dict[str, str],
   232	) -> dict[str, Any]:
   233	    required = dict(QWEN35_AUDITED_STACK_VERSIONS)
   234	    observed = {
   235	        package: package_versions.get(package, "missing") for package in sorted(required)
   236	    }
   237	    mismatches = {
   238	        package: {"expected": required[package], "observed": observed[package]}
   239	        for package in sorted(required)
   240	        if observed[package] != required[package]
   700	        raise RuntimeError(
   701	            "Qwen3.5 LoRA target coverage drifted: exact placement mismatch; "
   702	            f"expected_hash={placement['expected_target_module_set_sha256']}, "
   703	            f"actual_hash={placement['actual_target_module_set_sha256']}, "
   704	            f"unmatched={len(placement['unmatched_module_names'])}, "
   705	            f"duplicates={len(placement['duplicate_module_names'])}"
   706	        )
   707	    expected_trainable = expected_qwen35_trainable_parameters(rank)
   708	    if trainable_parameters != expected_trainable:
   709	        raise RuntimeError(
   710	            "Qwen3.5 trainable parameter count drifted: "
   711	            f"expected {expected_trainable}, got {trainable_parameters}"
   712	        )

   714	
   715	def sha256(path: Path) -> str:
   716	    digest = hashlib.sha256()
   717	    with path.open("rb") as handle:
   718	        for chunk in iter(lambda: handle.read(1024 * 1024), b""):
   719	            digest.update(chunk)
   720	    return digest.hexdigest()
   721	
   722	
   723	def sha256_bytes(value: bytes) -> str:
   724	    return hashlib.sha256(value).hexdigest()
   725	
   726	
   727	def canonical_json_sha256(value: Any) -> str:
   728	    encoded = json.dumps(
   729	        value, ensure_ascii=False, sort_keys=True, separators=(",", ":")
   730	    ).encode("utf-8")
   731	    return sha256_bytes(encoded)
   732	
   733	
   734	def read_once(path: Path, label: str) -> tuple[bytes, str]:
   735	    try:
   736	        value = path.read_bytes()
   737	    except OSError as error:
   738	        raise ValueError(f"Cannot read {label}: {path}") from error
   739	    return value, sha256_bytes(value)
   740	
   741	
   742	def parse_json_object_bytes(value: bytes, label: str) -> dict[str, Any]:
   743	    try:
   744	        text = value.decode("utf-8")
   745	    except UnicodeDecodeError as error:
   746	        raise ValueError(f"{label} is not valid UTF-8") from error
   747	    try:
   748	        parsed = json.loads(text)
   749	    except json.JSONDecodeError as error:
   750	        raise ValueError(f"{label} is invalid JSON") from error
   751	    if not isinstance(parsed, dict):
   752	        raise ValueError(f"{label} must be a JSON object")
   753	    return parsed
   754	
   755	
   756	def read_prompt_bytes(value: bytes, label: str) -> str:
   757	    try:
   758	        text = value.decode("utf-8")
   759	    except UnicodeDecodeError as error:
   760	        raise ValueError(f"{label} is not valid UTF-8") from error
   761	    lines = [
   762	        line
   763	        for line in text.splitlines()
   764	        if not line.startswith("#")
   765	    ]
   766	    prompt = "\n".join(lines).strip()
   767	    if not prompt:
   768	        raise ValueError("Prompt is empty after provenance comments are removed")
   769	    return prompt
   770	
   771	
   772	def read_rows_bytes(value: bytes, label: str) -> list[dict[str, Any]]:
   773	    try:
   774	        text = value.decode("utf-8")
   775	    except UnicodeDecodeError as error:
   776	        raise ValueError(f"{label} is not valid UTF-8") from error
   777	    rows: list[dict[str, Any]] = []
   778	    for line_number, line in enumerate(text.splitlines(), 1):
   779	        if not line.strip():
   780	            continue
   781	        try:
   782	            row = json.loads(line)
   783	        except json.JSONDecodeError as error:
   784	            raise ValueError(f"{label}:{line_number}: invalid JSON") from error
   785	        if not isinstance(row, dict):
   786	            raise ValueError(f"{label}:{line_number}: expected a JSON object")
   787	        if not isinstance(row.get("input"), str) or not isinstance(row.get("output"), str):
   788	            raise ValueError(f"{label}:{line_number}: missing string input/output")
   789	        rows.append(row)
   790	    if not rows:
   791	        raise ValueError("Training dataset is empty")
   792	    return rows
   793	
   794	
   795	def capture_training_inputs(
   796	    data_path: Path, prompt_path: Path
   797	) -> tuple[list[dict[str, Any]], str, str, str]:
   798	    data_bytes, data_sha256 = read_once(data_path, "training dataset")
   799	    prompt_bytes, prompt_sha256 = read_once(prompt_path, "training prompt")
   800	    rows = read_rows_bytes(data_bytes, str(data_path))
   801	    system_prompt = read_prompt_bytes(prompt_bytes, str(prompt_path))
   802	    return rows, system_prompt, data_sha256, prompt_sha256
   803	
   804	
   805	def write_json(path: Path, value: Any) -> None:
   806	    path.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
   807	    descriptor, temporary_name = tempfile.mkstemp(
   808	        prefix=f".{path.name}.", dir=path.parent
   809	    )
   810	    try:
   811	        with os.fdopen(descriptor, "w", encoding="utf-8") as handle:
   812	            json.dump(value, handle, ensure_ascii=False, indent=2)
   813	            handle.write("\n")
   814	        os.replace(temporary_name, path)
   815	    except BaseException:
   816	        try:
   817	            os.unlink(temporary_name)
   818	        except FileNotFoundError:
   819	            pass
   820	        raise
   821	
   822	
   823	def revalidate_qwen35_artifacts_after_model_load(
   824	    *,
   825	    base_path: Path,
   826	    startup_receipt: dict[str, Any],
   827	    manifest_path: Path,
   828	    manifest: dict[str, Any],
   829	) -> None:
   830	    startup_receipt_sha256 = canonical_json_sha256(startup_receipt)
   831	    post_load_receipt_sha256: str | None = None
   832	    try:
   833	        post_load_receipt = validate_qwen35_base_artifacts(base_path)
   834	        post_load_receipt_sha256 = canonical_json_sha256(post_load_receipt)
   835	        if post_load_receipt != startup_receipt:
   836	            raise RuntimeError(
   837	                "Post-load Qwen3.5 artifact receipt differs from the startup receipt"
   838	            )
   839	    except Exception as error:
   840	        manifest["status"] = "blocked_post_load_artifact_revalidation_failed"
   841	        failure_receipt: dict[str, Any] = {
   842	            "status": "failed",
   843	            "phase": "post_model_load_pre_adapter_injection",
   844	            "startup_receipt_sha256": startup_receipt_sha256,
   845	            "error_type": type(error).__name__,
   846	            "error_message_sha256": hashlib.sha256(
   847	                str(error).encode("utf-8")
   848	            ).hexdigest(),
   849	            "failed_at_epoch": time.time(),
   850	        }
   851	        if post_load_receipt_sha256 is not None:
   852	            failure_receipt["post_load_receipt_sha256"] = post_load_receipt_sha256
   853	        manifest["post_load_artifact_revalidation_receipt"] = failure_receipt
   854	        write_json(manifest_path, manifest)
   855	        raise
   856	
   857	    manifest["status"] = "base_artifacts_revalidated_after_load_not_injected"
   858	    manifest["post_load_artifact_revalidation_receipt"] = {
   859	        "status": "passed_pre_adapter_injection",
   860	        "phase": "post_model_load_pre_adapter_injection",
   861	        "startup_receipt_sha256": startup_receipt_sha256,
   862	        "post_load_receipt_sha256": post_load_receipt_sha256,
   863	        "revalidated_at_epoch": time.time(),
   864	    }
   865	    write_json(manifest_path, manifest)
   866	
   867	
   868	def adapter_receipt(
   869	    model: Any, lora_module_names: list[str], trainable_parameters: int
   870	) -> dict[str, Any]:
   871	    return {
   872	        "matched_module_count": len(lora_module_names),
   873	        "matched_module_names": lora_module_names,
   874	        "target_suffix_counts": qwen35_target_suffix_counts(lora_module_names),
   875	        "forbidden_vision_mtp_matches": qwen35_forbidden_target_names(lora_module_names),
   876	        "trainable_parameter_count": trainable_parameters,
   877	        "total_parameter_count": sum(parameter.numel() for parameter in model.parameters()),
   878	    }
   879	
   880	
   881	def persist_qwen35_adapter_receipt_before_validation(
   882	    *,
   883	    manifest_path: Path,
   884	    manifest: dict[str, Any],
   885	    receipt: dict[str, Any],
   886	    lora_module_names: list[str],
   887	    expected_module_names: list[str],
   888	    trainable_parameters: int,
   889	    rank: int,
   890	) -> None:
   891	    manifest["status"] = "adapter_validation_pending_not_complete"
   892	    receipt["validation_status"] = "pending"
   893	    receipt.update(
   894	        qwen35_normalized_adapter_placement(
   895	            lora_module_names, expected_module_names
   896	        )
   897	    )
   898	    manifest["adapter_receipt"] = receipt
   899	    write_json(manifest_path, manifest)
   900	
   901	    try:
   902	        validate_qwen35_adapter_receipt(
   903	            lora_module_names,
   904	            expected_module_names,
   905	            trainable_parameters,
   906	            rank,
   907	        )
   908	    except RuntimeError as error:
   909	        manifest["status"] = "blocked_adapter_validation_failed"
   910	        receipt["validation_status"] = "failed"
   911	        receipt["validation_error_type"] = type(error).__name__
   912	        receipt["validation_error_message_sha256"] = hashlib.sha256(
   913	            str(error).encode("utf-8")
   914	        ).hexdigest()
   915	        injection_receipt = manifest.get("adapter_injection_receipt")
   916	        if isinstance(injection_receipt, dict):
   917	            injection_receipt["status"] = "returned_adapter_validation_failed"
   918	        write_json(manifest_path, manifest)
   919	        raise
   920	
   921	    manifest["status"] = "adapter_validation_passed_not_trained"
   922	    receipt["validation_status"] = "passed"
   923	    injection_receipt = manifest.get("adapter_injection_receipt")
   924	    if isinstance(injection_receipt, dict):
   925	        injection_receipt["status"] = "validated_complete_pre_training"
   926	        injection_receipt["validated_at_epoch"] = time.time()
   927	    write_json(manifest_path, manifest)
   928	
   929	
   930	def observed_global_step(trainer: Any) -> int | None:
   931	    raw_step = getattr(getattr(trainer, "state", None), "global_step", None)
   932	    try:
   933	        return int(raw_step) if raw_step is not None else None
   934	    except (TypeError, ValueError):
   935	        return None
   936	
   937	
   938	def persist_training_failure(
   939	    *,
   940	    manifest_path: Path,
   941	    manifest: dict[str, Any],
   942	    trainer: Any,
   943	    status: str,
   944	    phase: str,
   945	    error: BaseException,
   946	) -> None:
   947	    step = observed_global_step(trainer)
   948	    error_message = str(error)
   949	    manifest["status"] = status
   950	    manifest["observed_global_step"] = step
   951	    manifest["failure_receipt"] = {
   952	        "phase": phase,
   953	        "error_type": type(error).__name__,
   954	        "error_message_sha256": hashlib.sha256(error_message.encode("utf-8")).hexdigest(),
   955	        "observed_global_step": step,
   956	        "failed_at_epoch": time.time(),
   957	    }
   958	    write_json(manifest_path, manifest)
   959	
   960	
   961	def run_training_and_save_with_receipts(
   962	    *,
   963	    trainer: Any,
   964	    model: Any,
   965	    tokenizer: Any,
   966	    torch: Any,
   967	    manifest_path: Path,
   968	    manifest: dict[str, Any],
   969	    output_dir: Path,
   970	    preflight_only: bool,
   971	    skip_merge: bool,
   972	) -> None:
   973	    torch.cuda.reset_peak_memory_stats()
   974	    manifest["status"] = "training_in_progress_not_complete"
   975	    manifest["training_started_at_epoch"] = time.time()
   976	    manifest["observed_global_step"] = observed_global_step(trainer)
   977	    write_json(manifest_path, manifest)
   978	
   979	    training_started = time.perf_counter()
   980	    try:
   981	        statistics = trainer.train()
   982	    except BaseException as error:
   983	        manifest["training_elapsed_seconds"] = round(
   984	            time.perf_counter() - training_started, 3
   985	        )
   986	        persist_training_failure(
   987	            manifest_path=manifest_path,
   988	            manifest=manifest,
   989	            trainer=trainer,
   990	            status="training_failed_not_complete",
   991	            phase="trainer.train",
   992	            error=error,
   993	        )
   994	        raise
   995	
   996	    completed_steps = observed_global_step(trainer)
   997	    manifest["training_elapsed_seconds"] = round(time.perf_counter() - training_started, 3)
   998	    manifest["training_metrics"] = statistics.metrics
   999	    manifest["completed_steps"] = completed_steps
  1000	    manifest["observed_global_step"] = completed_steps
  1001	    manifest["peak_cuda_memory_allocated_bytes"] = torch.cuda.max_memory_allocated()
  1002	    manifest["peak_cuda_memory_reserved_bytes"] = torch.cuda.max_memory_reserved()
  1003	    required_steps = int(QWEN35_PREFLIGHT_CONTRACT["max_steps"])
  1004	    if preflight_only and completed_steps != required_steps:
  1005	        error = RuntimeError(
  1006	            f"Preflight must complete exactly {required_steps} step, got {completed_steps}"
  1007	        )
  1008	        persist_training_failure(
  1009	            manifest_path=manifest_path,
  1010	            manifest=manifest,
  1011	            trainer=trainer,
  1012	            status="blocked_completed_step_mismatch",
  1013	            phase="completed_step_validation",
  1014	            error=error,
  1015	        )
  1016	        raise error
  1017	
  1018	    adapter_dir = output_dir / "adapter"
  1019	    manifest["status"] = "training_complete_save_pending_not_complete"
  1020	    manifest["training_completed_at_epoch"] = time.time()
  1021	    manifest["adapter_save_receipt"] = {
  1022	        "status": "pending",
  1023	        "adapter_path": str(adapter_dir),
  1024	    }
  1025	    write_json(manifest_path, manifest)
  1026	
  1027	    try:
  1028	        model.save_pretrained(adapter_dir)
  1029	        tokenizer.save_pretrained(adapter_dir)
  1030	    except BaseException as error:
  1031	        persist_training_failure(
  1032	            manifest_path=manifest_path,
  1033	            manifest=manifest,
  1034	            trainer=trainer,
  1035	            status="adapter_save_failed_not_complete",
  1036	            phase="adapter_save",
  1037	            error=error,
  1038	        )
  1039	        raise
  1040

   130	def model_family(config: dict[str, Any]) -> str:
   131	    model_type = str(config.get("model_type", "")).casefold().replace("_", "")
   132	    architectures = " ".join(str(item) for item in config.get("architectures", []))
   133	    if "qwen35" in model_type or "qwen3_5" in architectures.casefold():
   134	        return QWEN35_FAMILY
   135	    if "gemma" in model_type or "gemma" in architectures.casefold():
   136	        return "gemma"
   137	    return "other"
   138	
   139	
   140	def resolve_training_mode(family: str, requested: str) -> str:
   141	    resolved = BF16_LORA_MODE if family == QWEN35_FAMILY else QLORA_MODE
   142	    if requested != "auto":
   143	        resolved = requested
   144	    if family == QWEN35_FAMILY and resolved == QLORA_MODE:
   145	        raise ValueError(
   146	            "Refusing Qwen3.5 QLoRA: Unsloth recommends BF16 LoRA because its "
   147	            "4-bit quantization differences are higher than normal"
   148	        )
   149	    if family != QWEN35_FAMILY and resolved == BF16_LORA_MODE:
   150	        raise ValueError("BF16 LoRA is currently implemented only for Qwen3.5")
   151	    return resolved
