  1299	            r=args.rank,
  1300	            lora_alpha=args.alpha,
  1301	            lora_dropout=0.05,
  1302	            bias="none",
  1303	            target_modules=[
  1304	                "q_proj",
  1305	                "k_proj",
  1306	                "v_proj",
  1307	                "o_proj",
  1308	                "gate_proj",
  1309	                "up_proj",
  1310	                "down_proj",
  1311	            ],
  1312	            use_gradient_checkpointing="unsloth",
  1313	            random_state=args.seed,
  1314	        )
  1315	
  1316	    lora_module_names = sorted(
  1317	        name for name, module in model.named_modules() if hasattr(module, "lora_A")
  1318	    )
  1319	    trainable_parameters = sum(
  1320	        parameter.numel() for parameter in model.parameters() if parameter.requires_grad
  1321	    )
  1322	    receipt = adapter_receipt(model, lora_module_names, trainable_parameters)
  1323	    if family == QWEN35_FAMILY:
  1324	        if expected_qwen35_target_names is None:
  1325	            raise RuntimeError("Qwen3.5 expected target placement was not derived")
  1326	        persist_qwen35_adapter_receipt_before_validation(
  1327	            manifest_path=output_dir / "training-manifest.json",
  1328	            manifest=manifest,
  1329	            receipt=receipt,
  1330	            lora_module_names=lora_module_names,
  1331	            expected_module_names=expected_qwen35_target_names,
  1332	            trainable_parameters=trainable_parameters,
  1333	            rank=args.rank,
  1334	        )
  1335	    else:
  1336	        manifest["adapter_receipt"] = receipt
  1337	        write_json(output_dir / "training-manifest.json", manifest)
  1338	
  1339	    def to_text(row: dict[str, Any]) -> dict[str, str]:
  1340	        messages = [
  1341	            {"role": "system", "content": system_prompt},
  1342	            {
  1343	                "role": "user",
  1344	                "content": f"<TRANSCRIPT>\n{row['input']}\n</TRANSCRIPT>",
  1345	            },
  1346	            {"role": "assistant", "content": row["output"]},
  1347	        ]
  1348	        return {
  1349	            "text": tokenizer.apply_chat_template(
  1350	                messages,
  1351	                tokenize=False,
  1352	                add_generation_prompt=False,
  1353	                enable_thinking=False,
  1354	            )
  1355	        }
  1356	
  1357	    rendered_rows = [to_text(row) for row in rows]
  1358	    response_marker = response_marker_for_family(family)
  1359	    response_marker_counts = [row["text"].count(response_marker) for row in rendered_rows]
  1360	    if args.preflight_only:
  1361	        try:
  1362	            response_marker, response_marker_counts = validate_response_markers(
  1363	                [row["text"] for row in rendered_rows], family
  1364	            )
  1365	        except ValueError as error:
  1366	            raise RuntimeError(str(error)) from error
  1367	    manifest["response_mask_receipt"] = {
  1368	        "marker": response_marker,
  1369	        "rendered_marker_counts": response_marker_counts,
  1370	    }
  1371	    dataset = Dataset.from_list(rendered_rows).shuffle(seed=args.seed)
  1372	    trainer_kwargs: dict[str, Any] = {
  1373	        "model": model,
  1374	        "train_dataset": dataset,
  1375	        "args": sft_config(
  1376	            SFTConfig,
  1377	            dataset_text_field="text",
  1378	            per_device_train_batch_size=args.micro_batch,
  1379	            gradient_accumulation_steps=args.gradient_accumulation,
  1380	            num_train_epochs=args.epochs,
  1381	            max_steps=(
  1382	                int(QWEN35_PREFLIGHT_CONTRACT["max_steps"])
  1383	                if args.preflight_only
  1384	                else -1
  1385	            ),
  1386	            learning_rate=args.lr,
  1387	            warmup_ratio=warmup_ratio,
  1388	            lr_scheduler_type=scheduler,
  1389	            logging_steps=1 if args.preflight_only else 10,
  1390	            optim="adamw_8bit",
  1391	            weight_decay=0.01,
  1392	            bf16=True,
  1393	            max_seq_length=args.max_seq,
  1394	            output_dir=str(output_dir / "checkpoints"),
  1395	            report_to="none",
  1396	            seed=args.seed,
  1397	            save_strategy="no" if args.preflight_only else "epoch",
  1398	        ),
  1399	    }
  1400	    trainer_parameters = set(inspect.signature(SFTTrainer.__init__).parameters)
  1401	    if "tokenizer" in trainer_parameters:
  1402	        trainer_kwargs["tokenizer"] = tokenizer
  1403	    elif "processing_class" in trainer_parameters:
  1404	        trainer_kwargs["processing_class"] = tokenizer
  1405	    else:
  1406	        raise RuntimeError("SFTTrainer accepts neither tokenizer nor processing_class")
  1407	    trainer = SFTTrainer(**trainer_kwargs)
  1408	
  1409	    if family == "gemma":
  1410	        trainer = train_on_responses_only(
  1411	            trainer,
  1412	            instruction_part="<|turn>user\n",
  1413	            response_part="<|turn>model\n",
  1414	        )
  1415	    else:
  1416	        trainer = train_on_responses_only(
  1417	            trainer,
  1418	            instruction_part="<|im_start|>user\n",
  1419	            response_part="<|im_start|>assistant\n",
  1420	        )
  1421	
  1422	    if args.preflight_only:
  1423	        labeled_token_counts: list[int] = []
  1424	        for sample_index in range(len(trainer.train_dataset)):
  1425	            sample = trainer.train_dataset[sample_index]
  1426	            if "labels" not in sample:
  1427	                raise RuntimeError(
  1428	                    f"Response-only loss mask produced no labels for sample {sample_index}"
  1429	                )
  1430	            labeled_tokens = sum(token != -100 for token in sample["labels"])
  1431	            if labeled_tokens <= 0:
  1432	                raise RuntimeError(
  1433	                    f"Response-only loss mask removed every token in sample {sample_index}"
  1434	                )
  1435	            labeled_token_counts.append(labeled_tokens)
  1436	        manifest["sample_0_labeled_tokens"] = labeled_token_counts[0]
  1437	        manifest["response_mask_receipt"]["labeled_token_counts"] = labeled_token_counts
  1438	    else:
  1439	        # Historical behavior: normal Gemma/older-Qwen runs only sample row 0.
  1440	        sample = trainer.train_dataset[0]
  1441	        if "labels" in sample:
  1442	            labeled_tokens = sum(token != -100 for token in sample["labels"])
  1443	            if labeled_tokens <= 0:
  1444	                raise RuntimeError("Response-only loss mask removed every token in sample 0")
  1445	            manifest["sample_0_labeled_tokens"] = labeled_tokens
  1446	    write_json(output_dir / "training-manifest.json", manifest)
  1447	
  1448	    run_training_and_save_with_receipts(
  1449	        trainer=trainer,
  1450	        model=model,
  1451	        tokenizer=tokenizer,
  1452	        torch=torch,
  1453	        manifest_path=output_dir / "training-manifest.json",
  1454	        manifest=manifest,
  1455	        output_dir=output_dir,
  1456	        preflight_only=args.preflight_only,
  1457	        skip_merge=args.skip_merge,
  1458	    )
  1459	    print(json.dumps(manifest, ensure_ascii=False, indent=2))
  1460	
  1461	
  1462	if __name__ == "__main__":
  1463	    main()
     1	#!/usr/bin/env python3
     2	"""Deterministically score audited two-item list development cases.
     3	
     4	This scorer is intentionally narrow. It checks only facts that the corpus can
     5	prove mechanically: exactly two bullet lines, no extra prose beyond an optional
     6	header, all audited required phrases, and no audited forbidden phrases. Human or
     7	model-assisted semantic review remains a separate gate.
     8	"""
     9	
    10	from __future__ import annotations
    11	
    12	import argparse
    13	import json
    14	import math
    15	import re
    16	import unicodedata
    17	from collections import Counter, defaultdict
    18	from pathlib import Path
    19	from typing import Any
    20	
    21	
    22	BULLET_RE = re.compile(r"^\s*[-*•]\s+\S")
    23	
    24	
    25	def parse_args() -> argparse.Namespace:
    26	    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser()
    27	    parser.add_argument("--corpus", nargs="+", required=True)
    28	    parser.add_argument("--candidates", nargs="+", required=True)
    29	    parser.add_argument("--out", required=True)
    30	    parser.add_argument("--model-id", help="Force multiple candidate shards into one model group")
    31	    return parser.parse_args()
    32	
    33	
    34	def read_jsonl(path: Path) -> list[dict[str, Any]]:
    35	    return [json.loads(line) for line in path.read_text(encoding="utf-8").splitlines() if line]
    36	
    37	
    38	def normalized(text: str) -> str:
    39	    value = unicodedata.normalize("NFKC", text).casefold()
    40	    value = value.replace("’", "'").replace("–", "-").replace("—", "-")
    41	    value = re.sub(r"[-_/]", " ", value)
    42	    value = re.sub(r"[^\w$%.']+", " ", value)
    43	    return " ".join(value.split())
    44	
    45	
    46	def contains_phrase(text: str, phrase: str) -> bool:
    47	    haystack = f" {normalized(text)} "
    48	    needle = normalized(phrase)
    49	    return bool(needle) and f" {needle} " in haystack
    50	
    51	
    52	def wilson(successes: int, total: int) -> tuple[float, float]:
    53	    if total == 0:
    54	        return 0.0, 0.0
    55	    z = 1.959963984540054
    56	    p = successes / total
    57	    denominator = 1 + z * z / total
    58	    center = (p + z * z / (2 * total)) / denominator
    59	    margin = z * math.sqrt((p * (1 - p) + z * z / (4 * total)) / total) / denominator
    60	    return center - margin, center + margin
    61	
    62	
    63	def structure_ok(output: str) -> tuple[bool, int, list[str]]:
    64	    lines = [line.strip() for line in output.splitlines() if line.strip()]
    65	    bullets = [line for line in lines if BULLET_RE.match(line)]
    66	    other = [line for line in lines if not BULLET_RE.match(line)]
    67	    bare_list = len(lines) == 2 and len(bullets) == 2
    68	    headed_list = (
    69	        len(lines) == 3
    70	        and not BULLET_RE.match(lines[0])
    71	        and (lines[0].endswith(":") or lines[0].endswith("—"))
    72	        and all(BULLET_RE.match(line) for line in lines[1:])
    73	    )
    74	    return bare_list or headed_list, len(bullets), other
    75	
    76	
    77	def main() -> None:
    78	    args = parse_args()
    79	    cases: dict[str, dict[str, Any]] = {}
    80	    for raw_path in args.corpus:
    81	        for case in read_jsonl(Path(raw_path)):
    82	            if case["id"] in cases:
    83	                raise SystemExit(f"Duplicate corpus id: {case['id']}")
    84	            cases[case["id"]] = case
    85	
    86	    grouped_rows: dict[str, list[dict[str, Any]]] = defaultdict(list)
    87	    grouped_paths: dict[str, list[str]] = defaultdict(list)
    88	    for raw_path in args.candidates:
    89	        path = Path(raw_path)
    90	        rows = read_jsonl(path)
    91	        if not rows:
    92	            raise SystemExit(f"Empty candidate file: {path}")
    93	        model_id = args.model_id or str(rows[0].get("model_id", path.stem))
    94	        grouped_rows[model_id].extend(rows)
    95	        grouped_paths[model_id].append(str(path))
    96	
    97	    results: dict[str, Any] = {"case_count": len(cases), "models": {}}
    98	    for model_id, rows in grouped_rows.items():
    99	        path_label = ", ".join(grouped_paths[model_id])
   100	        by_id = {row["id"]: row for row in rows}
   101	        if len(by_id) != len(rows):
   102	            raise SystemExit(f"{model_id}: duplicate candidate ids across {path_label}")
   103	        missing = sorted(set(cases) - set(by_id))
   104	        extra = sorted(set(by_id) - set(cases))
   105	        if missing or extra:
   106	            raise SystemExit(f"{model_id}: missing={missing}, extra={extra}")
   107	
   108	        details: list[dict[str, Any]] = []
   109	        breakdown: dict[str, Counter[str]] = defaultdict(Counter)
   110	        for case_id, case in cases.items():
   111	            row = by_id[case_id]
   112	            output = str(row.get("output", row.get("candidate", ""))).strip()
   113	            absent_required = [p for p in case["required"] if not contains_phrase(output, p)]
   114	            present_forbidden = [p for p in case["forbidden"] if contains_phrase(output, p)]
   115	            valid_structure, bullet_count, extra_lines = structure_ok(output)
   116	            strict = not absent_required and not present_forbidden and valid_structure
   117	            details.append(
   118	                {
   119	                    "id": case_id,
   120	                    "domain": case["domain"],
   121	                    "list_type": case["list_type"],
   122	                    "structure_ok": valid_structure,
   123	                    "bullet_count": bullet_count,
   124	                    "extra_lines": extra_lines,
   125	                    "absent_required": absent_required,
   126	                    "present_forbidden": present_forbidden,
   127	                    "strict": strict,
   128	                    "output": output,
   129	                }
   130	            )
   131	            for key in (f"domain:{case['domain']}", f"list_type:{case['list_type']}"):
   132	                breakdown[key]["total"] += 1
   133	                breakdown[key]["strict"] += int(strict)
   134	
   135	        strict_count = sum(item["strict"] for item in details)
   136	        low, high = wilson(strict_count, len(details))
   137	        results["models"][model_id] = {
   138	            "candidate_paths": grouped_paths[model_id],
   139	            "strict": strict_count,
   140	            "total": len(details),
   141	            "strict_rate": strict_count / len(details),
   142	            "wilson_95": [low, high],
   143	            "structure_ok": sum(item["structure_ok"] for item in details),
   144	            "required_ok": sum(not item["absent_required"] for item in details),
   145	            "forbidden_ok": sum(not item["present_forbidden"] for item in details),
   146	            "breakdown": {key: dict(value) for key, value in sorted(breakdown.items())},
   147	            "failures": [item for item in details if not item["strict"]],
   148	        }
   149	
   150	    output_path = Path(args.out)
   151	    output_path.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
   152	    output_path.write_text(json.dumps(results, ensure_ascii=False, indent=2) + "\n", encoding="utf-8")
   153	    print(json.dumps({key: {"strict": value["strict"], "total": value["total"]} for key, value in results["models"].items()}, indent=2))
   154	
   155	
   156	if __name__ == "__main__":
   157	    main()
     1	#!/usr/bin/env python3
     2	"""Mechanically score positive-list activation and prose-restraint outputs."""
     3	
     4	from __future__ import annotations
     5	
     6	import argparse
     7	import json
     8	import math
     9	import re
    10	from collections import defaultdict
    11	from pathlib import Path
    12	from typing import Any
    13	
    14	
    15	LIST_LINE_RE = re.compile(r"^\s*(?:[-*•]|\d+[.)])\s+\S")
    16	
    17	
    18	def parse_args() -> argparse.Namespace:
    19	    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser()
    20	    parser.add_argument("--positive-corpus", type=Path, required=True)
    21	    parser.add_argument("--trap-corpus", type=Path, required=True)
    22	    parser.add_argument("--positive-candidates", type=Path, nargs="+", required=True)
    23	    parser.add_argument("--trap-candidates", type=Path, nargs="+", required=True)
    24	    parser.add_argument("--out", type=Path, required=True)
    25	    return parser.parse_args()
    26	
    27	
    28	def rows(path: Path) -> list[dict[str, Any]]:
    29	    return [json.loads(line) for line in path.read_text(encoding="utf-8").splitlines() if line]
    30	
    31	
    32	def output_text(row: dict[str, Any]) -> str:
    33	    return str(row.get("output", row.get("candidate", ""))).strip()
    34	
    35	
    36	def list_lines(text: str) -> int:
    37	    return sum(bool(LIST_LINE_RE.match(line)) for line in text.splitlines())
    38	
    39	
    40	def wilson(successes: int, total: int) -> list[float]:
    41	    if total == 0:
    42	        return [0.0, 0.0]
    43	    z = 1.959963984540054
    44	    p = successes / total
    45	    denominator = 1 + z * z / total
    46	    center = (p + z * z / (2 * total)) / denominator
    47	    margin = z * math.sqrt((p * (1 - p) + z * z / (4 * total)) / total) / denominator
    48	    return [center - margin, center + margin]
    49	
    50	
    51	def group(paths: list[Path]) -> dict[str, dict[str, dict[str, Any]]]:
    52	    grouped: dict[str, dict[str, dict[str, Any]]] = defaultdict(dict)
    53	    for path in paths:
    54	        part = rows(path)
    55	        if not part:
    56	            raise ValueError(f"empty candidate file: {path}")
    57	        model_id = str(part[0].get("model_id", path.stem))
    58	        for row in part:
    59	            row_id = str(row["id"])
    60	            if row_id in grouped[model_id]:
    61	                raise ValueError(f"duplicate candidate id {row_id!r} for {model_id}")
    62	            grouped[model_id][row_id] = row
    63	    return grouped
    64	
    65	
    66	def main() -> None:
    67	    args = parse_args()
    68	    positive = {str(row["id"]): row for row in rows(args.positive_corpus)}
    69	    traps = {str(row["id"]): row for row in rows(args.trap_corpus)}
    70	    positive_groups = group(args.positive_candidates)
    71	    trap_groups = group(args.trap_candidates)
    72	    if set(positive_groups) != set(trap_groups):
    73	        raise ValueError("positive and trap candidate model sets differ")
    74	
    75	    report: dict[str, Any] = {
    76	        "positive_corpus": str(args.positive_corpus),
    77	        "trap_corpus": str(args.trap_corpus),
    78	        "models": {},
    79	    }
    80	    for model_id in sorted(positive_groups):
    81	        positive_rows = positive_groups[model_id]
    82	        trap_rows = trap_groups[model_id]
    83	        if set(positive_rows) != set(positive):
    84	            raise ValueError(f"{model_id}: positive IDs do not match corpus")
    85	        if set(trap_rows) != set(traps):
    86	            raise ValueError(f"{model_id}: trap IDs do not match corpus")
    87	
    88	        positive_details = []
    89	        for row_id, case in positive.items():
    90	            count = list_lines(output_text(positive_rows[row_id]))
    91	            intended = int(case["item_count"])
    92	            positive_details.append(
    93	                {
    94	                    "id": row_id,
    95	                    "list_lines": count,
    96	                    "intended_items": intended,
    97	                    "activated": count >= 2,
    98	                    "intended_count": count == intended,
    99	                }
   100	            )
   101	        trap_details = []
   102	        for row_id in traps:
   103	            count = list_lines(output_text(trap_rows[row_id]))
   104	            trap_details.append({"id": row_id, "list_lines": count, "false_list": count >= 2})
   105	
   106	        activated = sum(item["activated"] for item in positive_details)
   107	        intended_count = sum(item["intended_count"] for item in positive_details)
   108	        false_lists = sum(item["false_list"] for item in trap_details)
   109	        report["models"][model_id] = {
   110	            "positive_total": len(positive_details),
   111	            "activated": activated,
   112	            "activated_wilson_95": wilson(activated, len(positive_details)),
   113	            "intended_count": intended_count,
   114	            "intended_count_wilson_95": wilson(intended_count, len(positive_details)),
   115	            "trap_total": len(trap_details),
   116	            "false_lists": false_lists,
   117	            "restraint": len(trap_details) - false_lists,
   118	            "restraint_wilson_95": wilson(len(trap_details) - false_lists, len(trap_details)),
   119	            "positive_mismatches": [item for item in positive_details if not item["intended_count"]],
   120	            "false_list_cases": [item for item in trap_details if item["false_list"]],
   121	        }
   122	
   123	    args.out.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
   124	    args.out.write_text(json.dumps(report, ensure_ascii=False, indent=2) + "\n", encoding="utf-8")
   125	    print(
   126	        json.dumps(
   127	            {
   128	                model: {
   129	                    "activated": score["activated"],
   130	                    "intended_count": score["intended_count"],
   131	                    "false_lists": score["false_lists"],
   132	                }
   133	                for model, score in report["models"].items()
   134	            },
   135	            indent=2,
   136	        )
   137	    )
   138	
   139	
   140	if __name__ == "__main__":
   141	    main()
     1	#!/usr/bin/env python3
     2	"""Score model-blind English list development cases deterministically.
     3	
     4	This scorer checks only audited spans and visible list structure. It does not
     5	provide semantic, native-speaker, frozen-benchmark, or release-quality proof.
     6	"""
     7	
     8	from __future__ import annotations
     9	
    10	import argparse
    11	import hashlib
    12	import json
    13	import math
    14	import os
    15	import re
    16	import tempfile
    17	import unicodedata
    18	from collections import defaultdict
    19	from pathlib import Path
    20	from typing import Any, Callable
    21	
    22	
    23	BULLET_RE = re.compile(r"^\s*[-*•]\s+(\S.*)$")
    24	NUMBER_RE = re.compile(r"^\s*(\d+)[.)]\s+(\S.*)$")
    25	AXES = ("domain", "case_type", "item_count", "length_bucket", "compound_required")
    26	
    27	
    28	def publish_exclusive(
    29	    path: Path, value: bytes, before_link: Callable[[], None] | None = None
    30	) -> None:
    31	    fd, temp_name = tempfile.mkstemp(prefix=f".{path.name}.", dir=path.parent)
    32	    temp = Path(temp_name)
    33	    try:
    34	        with os.fdopen(fd, "wb") as handle:
    35	            handle.write(value)
    36	            handle.flush()
    37	            os.fsync(handle.fileno())
    38	        if before_link is not None:
    39	            before_link()
    40	        os.link(temp, path)
    41	    finally:
    42	        temp.unlink(missing_ok=True)
    43	
    44	
    45	def parse_args() -> argparse.Namespace:
    46	    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser()
    47	    parser.add_argument("--positive-corpus", type=Path, required=True)
    48	    parser.add_argument("--restraint-corpus", type=Path, required=True)
    49	    parser.add_argument("--candidates", type=Path, nargs="+", required=True)
    50	    parser.add_argument("--out", type=Path, required=True)
    51	    return parser.parse_args()
    52	
    53	
    54	def read_jsonl(path: Path) -> list[dict[str, Any]]:
    55	    rows: list[dict[str, Any]] = []
    56	    with path.open(encoding="utf-8") as handle:
    57	        for line_number, line in enumerate(handle, 1):
    58	            if not line.strip():
    59	                continue
    60	            row = json.loads(line)
    61	            if not isinstance(row, dict):
    62	                raise ValueError(f"{path}:{line_number}: row is not an object")
    63	            rows.append(row)
    64	    if not rows:
    65	        raise ValueError(f"empty JSONL: {path}")
    66	    return rows
    67	
    68	
    69	def file_sha256(path: Path) -> str:
    70	    digest = hashlib.sha256()
    71	    with path.open("rb") as handle:
    72	        for chunk in iter(lambda: handle.read(1024 * 1024), b""):
    73	            digest.update(chunk)
    74	    return digest.hexdigest()
    75	
    76	
    77	def file_inventory(path: Path) -> dict[str, str]:
    78	    return {"path": str(path), "sha256": file_sha256(path)}
    79	
    80	
    81	def verify_inventory(inventory: dict[str, str]) -> None:
    82	    if file_sha256(Path(inventory["path"])) != inventory["sha256"]:
    83	        raise RuntimeError(f"source changed during scoring: {inventory['path']}")
    84	
    85	
    86	def normalized(text: str) -> str:
    87	    value = unicodedata.normalize("NFKC", text).casefold()
    88	    value = value.replace("’", "'").replace("–", "-").replace("—", "-")
    89	    value = re.sub(r"[-_/]", " ", value)
    90	    value = re.sub(r"[^\w$%]+", " ", value, flags=re.UNICODE)
    91	    return " ".join(value.split())
    92	
    93	
    94	def contains_phrase(text: str, phrase: str) -> bool:
    95	    haystack = f" {normalized(text)} "
    96	    needle = normalized(phrase)
    97	    return bool(needle) and f" {needle} " in haystack
    98	
    99	
   100	def wilson(successes: int, total: int) -> list[float] | None:
   101	    if total == 0:
   102	        return None
   103	    z = 1.959963984540054
   104	    rate = successes / total
   105	    denominator = 1 + z * z / total
   106	    center = (rate + z * z / (2 * total)) / denominator
   107	    margin = z * math.sqrt((rate * (1 - rate) + z * z / (4 * total)) / total) / denominator
   108	    return [center - margin, center + margin]
   109	
   110	
   111	def metric(successes: int, total: int) -> dict[str, Any]:
   112	    return {
   113	        "successes": successes,
   114	        "total": total,
   115	        "rate": successes / total if total else None,
   116	        "wilson_95": wilson(successes, total),
   117	    }
   118	
   119	
   120	def load_corpus(path: Path, role: str) -> dict[str, dict[str, Any]]:
   121	    cases: dict[str, dict[str, Any]] = {}
   122	    for line_number, case in enumerate(read_jsonl(path), 1):
   123	        case_id = case.get("id")
   124	        if not isinstance(case_id, str) or not case_id:
   125	            raise ValueError(f"{path}:{line_number}: missing id")
   126	        if case_id in cases:
   127	            raise ValueError(f"{path}:{line_number}: duplicate corpus id {case_id!r}")
   128	        if (
   129	            case.get("split") != "dev"
   130	            or case.get("gold_status") != "candidate_unreviewed"
   131	            or case.get("native_reviewed") is not False
   132	            or case.get("training_eligible") is not False
   133	        ):
   134	            raise ValueError(f"{case_id}: corpus is not development/unreviewed/non-training data")
   135	        if case.get("benchmark_role") != role:
   136	            raise ValueError(f"{case_id}: expected benchmark_role {role!r}")
   137	        for field in ("domain", "case_type", "length_bucket"):
   138	            if not isinstance(case.get(field), str) or not case[field]:
   139	                raise ValueError(f"{case_id}: invalid {field}")
   140	        if not isinstance(case.get("item_count"), int) or case["item_count"] < 1:
   141	            raise ValueError(f"{case_id}: invalid item_count")
   142	        if not isinstance(case.get("compound_required"), bool):
   143	            raise ValueError(f"{case_id}: invalid compound_required")
   144	        for field in ("items", "compound_items", "scope_anchors", "forbidden"):
   145	            if not isinstance(case.get(field), list) or not all(
   146	                isinstance(value, str) and value.strip() for value in case[field]
   147	            ):
   148	                raise ValueError(f"{case_id}: invalid {field}")
   149	        if len(case["items"]) != case["item_count"] or not case["scope_anchors"]:
   150	            raise ValueError(f"{case_id}: audited item/scope coverage is invalid")
   151	        normalized_items = {normalized(value) for value in case["items"]}
   152	        normalized_compounds = {normalized(value) for value in case["compound_items"]}
   153	        if (
   154	            len(normalized_items) != case["item_count"]
   155	            or not normalized_compounds.issubset(normalized_items)
   156	            or case["compound_required"] != bool(case["compound_items"])
   157	        ):
   158	            raise ValueError(f"{case_id}: audited compound/item contract is invalid")
   159	        expected_format = case.get("expected_formatting")
   160	        allowed = {"bullets", "numbered"} if role == "positive_list" else {"prose"}
   161	        if expected_format not in allowed:
   162	            raise ValueError(f"{case_id}: invalid expected_formatting {expected_format!r}")
   163	        cases[case_id] = case
   164	    return cases
   165	
   166	
   167	def candidate_text(row: dict[str, Any]) -> str:
   168	    value = row.get("output", row.get("candidate"))
   169	    return value.strip() if isinstance(value, str) else ""
   170	
   171	
   172	def candidate_error(row: dict[str, Any]) -> Any | None:
   173	    value = row.get("error")
   174	    return None if value is None or value == "" else value
   175	
   176	
   177	def score_candidate(
   178	    case: dict[str, Any],
   179	    row: dict[str, Any],
   180	    scorer: Callable[[dict[str, Any], str], dict[str, Any]],
   181	) -> dict[str, Any]:
   182	    output = candidate_text(row)
   183	    result = scorer(case, output)
   184	    error = candidate_error(row)
   185	    inference_ok = error is None and bool(output)
   186	    result["candidate_error"] = error
   187	    result["empty_output"] = not bool(output)
   188	    result["inference_ok"] = inference_ok
   189	    if not inference_ok:
   190	        result["strict"] = False
   191	    return result
   192	
   193	
   194	def load_candidates(paths: list[Path]) -> tuple[dict[str, dict[str, dict[str, Any]]], dict[str, list[str]]]:
   195	    grouped: dict[str, dict[str, dict[str, Any]]] = defaultdict(dict)
   196	    sources: dict[str, list[str]] = defaultdict(list)
   197	    for path in paths:
   198	        rows = read_jsonl(path)
   199	        models_in_file: set[str] = set()
   200	        for line_number, row in enumerate(rows, 1):
   201	            model_id = row.get("model_id", path.stem)
   202	            case_id = row.get("id")
   203	            if not isinstance(model_id, str) or not model_id:
   204	                raise ValueError(f"{path}:{line_number}: invalid model_id")
   205	            if not isinstance(case_id, str) or not case_id:
   206	                raise ValueError(f"{path}:{line_number}: invalid id")
   207	            if case_id in grouped[model_id]:
   208	                raise ValueError(f"{model_id}: duplicate candidate id {case_id!r}")
   209	            grouped[model_id][case_id] = row
   210	            models_in_file.add(model_id)
   211	        for model_id in models_in_file:
   212	            sources[model_id].append(str(path))
   213	    return dict(grouped), {key: sorted(value) for key, value in sources.items()}
   214	
   215	
   216	def line_structure(output: str) -> dict[str, Any]:
   217	    lines = [line.strip() for line in output.splitlines() if line.strip()]
   218	    bullets: list[str] = []
   219	    numbered: list[tuple[int, str]] = []
   220	    prose: list[str] = []
   221	    for line in lines:
   222	        bullet = BULLET_RE.match(line)
   223	        number = NUMBER_RE.match(line)
   224	        if bullet:
   225	            bullets.append(bullet.group(1))
   226	        elif number:
   227	            numbered.append((int(number.group(1)), number.group(2)))
   228	        else:
   229	            prose.append(line)
   230	    return {"lines": lines, "bullets": bullets, "numbered": numbered, "prose": prose}

     1	#!/usr/bin/env python3
     2	"""Deterministically score audited two-item list development cases.
     3	
     4	This scorer is intentionally narrow. It checks only facts that the corpus can
     5	prove mechanically: exactly two bullet lines, no extra prose beyond an optional
     6	header, all audited required phrases, and no audited forbidden phrases. Human or
     7	model-assisted semantic review remains a separate gate.
     8	"""
     9	
    10	from __future__ import annotations
    11	
    12	import argparse
    13	import json
    14	import math
    15	import re
    16	import unicodedata
    17	from collections import Counter, defaultdict
    18	from pathlib import Path
    19	from typing import Any
    20	
    21	
    22	BULLET_RE = re.compile(r"^\s*[-*•]\s+\S")
    23	
    24	
    25	def parse_args() -> argparse.Namespace:
    26	    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser()
    27	    parser.add_argument("--corpus", nargs="+", required=True)
    28	    parser.add_argument("--candidates", nargs="+", required=True)
    29	    parser.add_argument("--out", required=True)
    30	    parser.add_argument("--model-id", help="Force multiple candidate shards into one model group")
    31	    return parser.parse_args()
    32	
    33	
    34	def read_jsonl(path: Path) -> list[dict[str, Any]]:
    35	    return [json.loads(line) for line in path.read_text(encoding="utf-8").splitlines() if line]
    36	
    37	
    38	def normalized(text: str) -> str:
    39	    value = unicodedata.normalize("NFKC", text).casefold()
    40	    value = value.replace("’", "'").replace("–", "-").replace("—", "-")
    41	    value = re.sub(r"[-_/]", " ", value)
    42	    value = re.sub(r"[^\w$%.']+", " ", value)
    43	    return " ".join(value.split())
    44	
    45	
    46	def contains_phrase(text: str, phrase: str) -> bool:
    47	    haystack = f" {normalized(text)} "
    48	    needle = normalized(phrase)
    49	    return bool(needle) and f" {needle} " in haystack
    50	
    51	
    52	def wilson(successes: int, total: int) -> tuple[float, float]:
    53	    if total == 0:
    54	        return 0.0, 0.0
    55	    z = 1.959963984540054
    56	    p = successes / total
    57	    denominator = 1 + z * z / total
    58	    center = (p + z * z / (2 * total)) / denominator
    59	    margin = z * math.sqrt((p * (1 - p) + z * z / (4 * total)) / total) / denominator
    60	    return center - margin, center + margin
    61	
    62	
    63	def structure_ok(output: str) -> tuple[bool, int, list[str]]:
    64	    lines = [line.strip() for line in output.splitlines() if line.strip()]
    65	    bullets = [line for line in lines if BULLET_RE.match(line)]
    66	    other = [line for line in lines if not BULLET_RE.match(line)]
    67	    bare_list = len(lines) == 2 and len(bullets) == 2
    68	    headed_list = (
    69	        len(lines) == 3
    70	        and not BULLET_RE.match(lines[0])
    71	        and (lines[0].endswith(":") or lines[0].endswith("—"))
    72	        and all(BULLET_RE.match(line) for line in lines[1:])
    73	    )
    74	    return bare_list or headed_list, len(bullets), other
    75	
    76	
    77	def main() -> None:
    78	    args = parse_args()
    79	    cases: dict[str, dict[str, Any]] = {}
    80	    for raw_path in args.corpus:
    81	        for case in read_jsonl(Path(raw_path)):
    82	            if case["id"] in cases:
    83	                raise SystemExit(f"Duplicate corpus id: {case['id']}")
    84	            cases[case["id"]] = case
    85	
    86	    grouped_rows: dict[str, list[dict[str, Any]]] = defaultdict(list)
    87	    grouped_paths: dict[str, list[str]] = defaultdict(list)
    88	    for raw_path in args.candidates:
    89	        path = Path(raw_path)
    90	        rows = read_jsonl(path)
    91	        if not rows:
    92	            raise SystemExit(f"Empty candidate file: {path}")
    93	        model_id = args.model_id or str(rows[0].get("model_id", path.stem))
    94	        grouped_rows[model_id].extend(rows)
    95	        grouped_paths[model_id].append(str(path))
    96	
    97	    results: dict[str, Any] = {"case_count": len(cases), "models": {}}
    98	    for model_id, rows in grouped_rows.items():
    99	        path_label = ", ".join(grouped_paths[model_id])
   100	        by_id = {row["id"]: row for row in rows}
   101	        if len(by_id) != len(rows):
   102	            raise SystemExit(f"{model_id}: duplicate candidate ids across {path_label}")
   103	        missing = sorted(set(cases) - set(by_id))
   104	        extra = sorted(set(by_id) - set(cases))
   105	        if missing or extra:
   106	            raise SystemExit(f"{model_id}: missing={missing}, extra={extra}")
   107	
   108	        details: list[dict[str, Any]] = []
   109	        breakdown: dict[str, Counter[str]] = defaultdict(Counter)
   110	        for case_id, case in cases.items():
   111	            row = by_id[case_id]
   112	            output = str(row.get("output", row.get("candidate", ""))).strip()
   113	            absent_required = [p for p in case["required"] if not contains_phrase(output, p)]
   114	            present_forbidden = [p for p in case["forbidden"] if contains_phrase(output, p)]
   115	            valid_structure, bullet_count, extra_lines = structure_ok(output)
   116	            strict = not absent_required and not present_forbidden and valid_structure
   117	            details.append(
   118	                {
   119	                    "id": case_id,
   120	                    "domain": case["domain"],
   121	                    "list_type": case["list_type"],
   122	                    "structure_ok": valid_structure,
   123	                    "bullet_count": bullet_count,
   124	                    "extra_lines": extra_lines,
   125	                    "absent_required": absent_required,
   126	                    "present_forbidden": present_forbidden,
   127	                    "strict": strict,
   128	                    "output": output,
   129	                }
   130	            )
   131	            for key in (f"domain:{case['domain']}", f"list_type:{case['list_type']}"):
   132	                breakdown[key]["total"] += 1
   133	                breakdown[key]["strict"] += int(strict)
   134	
   135	        strict_count = sum(item["strict"] for item in details)
   136	        low, high = wilson(strict_count, len(details))
   137	        results["models"][model_id] = {
   138	            "candidate_paths": grouped_paths[model_id],
   139	            "strict": strict_count,
   140	            "total": len(details),
   141	            "strict_rate": strict_count / len(details),
   142	            "wilson_95": [low, high],
   143	            "structure_ok": sum(item["structure_ok"] for item in details),
   144	            "required_ok": sum(not item["absent_required"] for item in details),
   145	            "forbidden_ok": sum(not item["present_forbidden"] for item in details),
   146	            "breakdown": {key: dict(value) for key, value in sorted(breakdown.items())},
   147	            "failures": [item for item in details if not item["strict"]],
   148	        }
   149	
   150	    output_path = Path(args.out)
   151	    output_path.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
   152	    output_path.write_text(json.dumps(results, ensure_ascii=False, indent=2) + "\n", encoding="utf-8")
   153	    print(json.dumps({key: {"strict": value["strict"], "total": value["total"]} for key, value in results["models"].items()}, indent=2))
   154	
   155

   157	    main()

    66	def sha256_bytes(value: bytes) -> str:
    67	    return hashlib.sha256(value).hexdigest()
    68	
    69	
    70	def read_once(path: Path, label: str) -> tuple[bytes, str]:
    71	    try:
    72	        value = path.read_bytes()
    73	    except OSError as error:
    74	        raise ValueError(f"cannot read {label}") from error
    75	    return value, sha256_bytes(value)

    77	
    78	def parse_json(value: bytes, label: str) -> dict[str, Any]:
    79	    try:
    80	        parsed = json.loads(value)
    81	    except (UnicodeDecodeError, json.JSONDecodeError) as error:
    82	        raise ValueError(f"{label} is invalid JSON") from error
    83	    if not isinstance(parsed, dict):
    84	        raise ValueError(f"{label} is not an object")
    85	    return parsed
    86	
    87	
    88	def parse_jsonl(value: bytes, label: str) -> list[dict[str, Any]]:
    89	    try:
    90	        lines = value.decode("utf-8").splitlines()
    91	    except UnicodeDecodeError as error:
    92	        raise ValueError(f"{label} is not UTF-8") from error
    93	    rows: list[dict[str, Any]] = []
    94	    for line_number, line in enumerate(lines, 1):
    95	        if not line.strip():
    96	            continue
    97	        try:
    98	            row = json.loads(line)
    99	        except json.JSONDecodeError as error:
   100	            raise ValueError(f"{label}:{line_number} is invalid JSON") from error
   101	        if not isinstance(row, dict):
   102	            raise ValueError(f"{label}:{line_number} is not an object")
   103	        rows.append(row)
   104	    return rows
   105	
   106	
   107	def require_sha(actual: str, expected: Any, label: str) -> None:
   108	    if not isinstance(expected, str) or not re.fullmatch(r"[0-9a-f]{64}", expected):
   109	        raise ValueError(f"expected {label} SHA-256 is invalid")
   110	    if actual != expected:
   111	        raise ValueError(f"{label} differs from its bound SHA-256")
   112	
   113	
   114	def resolve_recorded_path(value: Any, label: str) -> Path:
   115	    if not isinstance(value, str) or not value:
   116	        raise ValueError(f"{label} path is invalid")
   117	    path = Path(value)
   118	    if path.is_absolute():
   119	        return path
   120	    if ".." in path.parts:
   121	        raise ValueError(f"{label} path escapes the repository")
   122	    return REPO_ROOT / path
   123	
   124	
   125	def direct_child(bundle: Path, value: Any, label: str) -> Path:
   126	    if not isinstance(value, str) or not value:
   127	        raise ValueError(f"{label} path is invalid")
   128	    relative = Path(value)
   129	    if relative.is_absolute() or ".." in relative.parts or len(relative.parts) != 1:
   130	        raise ValueError(f"{label} path is not a direct bundle child")
   131	    path = bundle / relative
   132	    if not path.is_file() or path.is_symlink():
   133	        raise ValueError(f"{label} is unavailable")
   134	    return path
   135	
   136	
   137	def encode_jsonl(rows: list[dict[str, Any]]) -> bytes:
   138	    return b"".join(
   139	        (json.dumps(row, ensure_ascii=False, sort_keys=True) + "\n").encode("utf-8")
   140	        for row in rows
   141	    )
   142	
   143	
   144	def write_exclusive(path: Path, value: bytes) -> None:
   145	    with path.open("xb") as handle:
   146	        handle.write(value)
   147	        handle.flush()
   148	        os.fsync(handle.fileno())
   149	
   150	
   151	def corpus_rows(value: bytes, label: str, seen: set[str]) -> list[dict[str, Any]]:
   152	    rows = parse_jsonl(value, label)
   153	    if len(rows) != EXPECTED_LANE_CASES:
   154	        raise ValueError(f"{label} must contain exactly {EXPECTED_LANE_CASES} cases")
   155	    for row in rows:
   156	        case_id = row.get("id")
   157	        transcript = row.get("input")
   158	        if not isinstance(case_id, str) or not case_id or case_id in seen:
   159	            raise ValueError("corpus has an invalid or duplicate case ID")
   160	        if not isinstance(transcript, str) or not transcript.strip():
   161	            raise ValueError(f"{case_id}: corpus has an invalid input")
   162	        seen.add(case_id)
   163	    return rows
   164	
   165	
   166	def outputs_by_id(value: bytes, expected_ids: list[str], arm: str) -> dict[str, str]:
   167	    rows = parse_jsonl(value, f"{arm} output")
   168	    ids = [row.get("id") for row in rows]
   169	    if ids != expected_ids or len(set(ids)) != len(ids):
   170	        raise ValueError(f"{arm} output IDs/order differ from the corpora")
   171	    outputs: dict[str, str] = {}
   172	    for row in rows:
   173	        value = row.get("candidate")
   174	        if not isinstance(value, str) or not value.strip() or row.get("error") not in (None, ""):
   175	            raise ValueError(f"{arm}:{row.get('id')} is not reviewable")
   176	        outputs[row["id"]] = value
   177	    return outputs

   134	def validate_judgment(row: dict[str, Any]) -> tuple[str, str]:
   135	    expected_fields = {"case_id", "label", "meaning_damage", "severity", "tags", "note"}
   136	    if set(row) != expected_fields:
   137	        raise ValueError("judgment fields are invalid")
   138	    case_id = row["case_id"]
   139	    label = row["label"]
   140	    if not isinstance(case_id, str) or not case_id or label not in LABELS:
   141	        raise ValueError("judgment case/label is invalid")
   142	    if (
   143	        type(row["meaning_damage"]) is not bool
   144	        or not isinstance(row["severity"], str)
   145	        or row["severity"] not in SEVERITY
   146	    ):
   147	        raise ValueError(f"{case_id}/{label}: judgment damage/severity is invalid")
   148	    if row["meaning_damage"] != (SEVERITY[row["severity"]] >= 2):
   149	        raise ValueError(f"{case_id}/{label}: damage flag disagrees with severity")
   150	    if (
   151	        not isinstance(row["tags"], list)
   152	        or any(not isinstance(tag, str) for tag in row["tags"])
   153	        or len(set(row["tags"])) != len(row["tags"])
   154	        or any(tag not in TAGS for tag in row["tags"])
   155	    ):
   156	        raise ValueError(f"{case_id}/{label}: tags are invalid")
   157	    if not isinstance(row["note"], str) or not row["note"].strip():
   158	        raise ValueError(f"{case_id}/{label}: note is invalid")
   159	    return case_id, label
   160	
   161	
   162	def verify_bound_files(bindings: dict[str, str]) -> list[tuple[Path, str, str]]:
   163	    sources: list[tuple[Path, str, str]] = []
   164	    for key, path in BOUND_HASH_PATHS.items():
   165	        _, actual = read_once(path, key)
   166	        require_binding(bindings, key, actual)
   167	        sources.append((path, actual, key))
   168	    return sources
   169	
   170	
   171	def validate_contract_receipt(
   172	    receipt: dict[str, Any],
   173	) -> tuple[str, dict[str, str], str, list[tuple[Path, str, str]]]:
   174	    _, contract_sha, bindings = load_contract(CANONICAL_DECISION_CONTRACT)
   175	    execution_head = validate_binding_commit(
   176	        bindings, CANONICAL_DECISION_CONTRACT, REPO_ROOT
   177	    )
   178	    record = receipt.get("decision_contract")
   179	    if not isinstance(record, dict):
   180	        raise ValueError("packet receipt lacks the executable decision contract")

   184	
   185	
   186	def load_audit_sources(paths: list[str]) -> tuple[list[AuditInput], list[dict[str, Any]], set[str]]:
   187	    audit_inputs: list[AuditInput] = []
   188	    inventories: list[dict[str, Any]] = []
   189	    families: set[str] = set()
   190	    for raw_path in paths:
   191	        path = Path(raw_path).resolve()
   192	        if not path.is_file():
   193	            raise ValueError(f"audit source does not exist: {path}")
   194	        source_bytes = path.read_bytes()
   195	        rows = load_json_rows_bytes(path, source_bytes)
   196	        source_sha = sha256_bytes(source_bytes)
   197	        input_count = 0
   198	        for index, row in enumerate(rows, 1):
   199	            family = row.get("family")
   200	            if isinstance(family, str) and family.strip():
   201	                families.add(normalized(family))
   202	            value = input_value(row)
   203	            if value is None:
   204	                continue
   205	            key = normalized(value)
   206	            if not key:
   207	                continue
   208	            input_count += 1
   209	            audit_inputs.append(
   210	                AuditInput(
   211	                    source=str(path),
   212	                    source_id=str(row.get("id", index)),
   213	                    normalized=key,
   214	                    tokens=frozenset(key.split()),
   215	                    char_4grams=char_ngrams(key),
   216	                )
   217	            )
   218	        inventories.append(
   219	            {
   220	                "path": str(path),
   221	                "sha256": source_sha,
   222	                "row_count": len(rows),
   223	                "input_count": input_count,
   224	            }
   225	        )
   226	    if not audit_inputs:
   227	        raise ValueError("audit sources contain no input/asr_input/raw/transcript values")
   228	    return audit_inputs, inventories, families
   229	
   230	
   231	def pairwise_balance(specs: list[CaseSpec]) -> dict[str, dict[str, int]]:
   232	    axes = ("domain", "case_type", "item_count", "length_bucket", "compound_required")
   233	    axis_values = {
   234	        axis: sorted({getattr(spec, axis) for spec in specs}, key=str)
   235	        for axis in axes
   236	    }
   237	    result: dict[str, dict[str, int]] = {}
   238	    for left_index, left in enumerate(axes):
   239	        for right in axes[left_index + 1 :]:
   240	            counts: Counter[str] = Counter(
   241	                f"{getattr(spec, left)}|{getattr(spec, right)}" for spec in specs
   242	            )
   243	            result[f"{left}_x_{right}"] = {
   244	                f"{left_value}|{right_value}": counts[f"{left_value}|{right_value}"]
   245	                for left_value, right_value in product(axis_values[left], axis_values[right])
   246	            }
   247	    return result
   248	
   249	
   250	def balanced_specs(role: str, count: int, seed: int) -> list[CaseSpec]:
   251	    if count <= 0 or count % 20:
   252	        raise ValueError("--count-per-role must be a positive multiple of 20")
   253	    case_types = POSITIVE_TYPES if role == "positive_list" else RESTRAINT_TYPES
   254	    repeats = count // (len(DOMAINS) * len(case_types))
   255	    slots = [
   256	        (domain, case_type, repeat, bool((domain_index + type_index + repeat) % 2))
   257	        for domain_index, domain in enumerate(DOMAINS)
   258	        for type_index, case_type in enumerate(case_types)
   259	        for repeat in range(repeats)
   260	    ]
   261	    item_counts = list(ITEM_COUNTS)
   262	    length_buckets = list(LENGTH_BUCKETS)
   263	    rng = random.Random(seed)
   264	    candidate = [
   265	        CaseSpec(
   266	            spec_id="pending",
   267	            role=role,
   268	            domain=domain,
   269	            case_type=case_type,
   270	            item_count=item_counts[(repeat + 2 * domain_index + type_index) % 4],
   271	            length_bucket=length_buckets[(2 * repeat + domain_index + type_index) % 4],
   272	            compound_required=compound_required,
   273	        )
   274	        for domain_index, domain in enumerate(DOMAINS)
   275	        for type_index, case_type in enumerate(case_types)
   276	        for repeat, (_, _, _, compound_required) in enumerate(
   277	            slot for slot in slots if slot[0] == domain and slot[1] == case_type
   278	        )
   279	    ]
   280	    rng.shuffle(candidate)
   281	    return [
   282	        CaseSpec(spec_id=f"{role}-{index + 1:03d}", **{key: value for key, value in asdict(spec).items() if key != "spec_id"})
   283	        for index, spec in enumerate(candidate)
   284	    ]
   285

   287	def subscription_env() -> dict[str, str]:
   288	    return {
   289	        key: value
   290	        for key, value in os.environ.items()
   291	        if not key.startswith("ANTHROPIC_")
   292	        and key not in ("CLAUDE_CODE_USE_BEDROCK", "CLAUDE_CODE_USE_VERTEX")
   293	    }

   296	def call_claude(model: str, system: str, user: str, timeout_seconds: int) -> str:
   297	    try:
   298	        process = subprocess.run(
   299	            [
   300	                "claude",
   700	        system, user = generation_prompt(batch)
   701	        prompt_sha = sha256_bytes((system + "\n" + user).encode())
   702	        prompt_record = {
   703	            "role": role,
   704	            "batch": batch_index,
   705	            "spec_ids": [spec.spec_id for spec in batch],
   706	            "prompt_sha256": prompt_sha,
   707	            "reused_checkpoint": False,
   708	        }
   709	        prompt_records.append(prompt_record)
   710	        accepted: list[tuple[dict[str, Any], dict[str, Any]]] | None = None
   711	        last_error: Exception | None = None
   712	        checkpoint = checkpoint_dir / f"{role}-batch-{batch_index:03d}.json" if checkpoint_dir else None
   713	        if checkpoint and checkpoint.is_file():
   714	            cached = json.loads(checkpoint.read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
   715	            if (
   716	                cached.get("prompt_sha256") != prompt_sha
   717	                or cached.get("specs") != [asdict(spec) for spec in batch]
   718	                or not isinstance(cached.get("rows"), list)
   719	            ):
   720	                raise RuntimeError(f"checkpoint does not match current prompt/specs: {checkpoint}")
   721	            accepted = validate_batch(cached["rows"], batch, blocked, families)
   722	            prompt_record["reused_checkpoint"] = True
   723	        else:
   724	            for attempt in range(1, args.attempts_per_batch + 1):
   725	                try:
   726	                    raw_rows = clean_json(
   727	                        call_claude(args.model, system, user, args.call_timeout_seconds)
   728	                    )
   729	                    accepted = validate_batch(raw_rows, batch, blocked, families)
   730	                    if checkpoint:
   731	                        publish_bytes_exclusive(
   732	                            checkpoint,
   733	                            (
   734	                                json.dumps(
   735	                                    {
   736	                                        "prompt_sha256": prompt_sha,
   737	                                        "specs": [asdict(spec) for spec in batch],
   738	                                        "rows": raw_rows,
   739	                                    },
   740	                                    ensure_ascii=False,
   741	                                    indent=2,
   742	                                    sort_keys=True,
   743	                                )
   744	                                + "\n"
   745	                            ).encode("utf-8"),
   746	                        )
   747	                    break
   748	                except (ValueError, json.JSONDecodeError, RuntimeError) as error:
   749	                    last_error = error
   750	                    failures.append({
   751	                        "role": role,
   752	                        "batch": str(batch_index),
   753	                        "attempt": str(attempt),
   754	                        "reason": str(error)[:500],
   755	                    })
   756	                    print(f"{role} batch {batch_index} attempt {attempt} failed: {error}", flush=True)
   757	        if accepted is None:
   758	            raise RuntimeError(f"{role} batch {batch_index} failed after {args.attempts_per_batch} attempts: {last_error}")
   759	        for spec, (row, similarity_audit) in zip(batch, accepted):
   760	            enriched = enrich(row, spec, args.batch_id, similarity_audit)
   761	            rows.append(enriched)
   762	            families.add(normalized(row["family"]))
   763	            blocked.append(as_audit_input("generated", enriched["id"], row["input"]))
   764	        print(f"accepted {role} batch {batch_index}: {len(accepted)} rows", flush=True)
   765	    return rows, prompt_records, failures, blocked, families

   768	def ensure_source_snapshots_unchanged(inventories: list[dict[str, Any]]) -> None:
   769	    for inventory in inventories:
   770	        path = Path(inventory["path"])
   771	        if file_sha256(path) != inventory["sha256"]:
   772	            raise RuntimeError(f"audit source changed during generation: {path}")

   775	def manifest_path(args: argparse.Namespace) -> Path:
   776	    if args.manifest_output:
   777	        return Path(args.manifest_output).resolve()
   778	    positive = Path(args.positive_output).resolve()
   779	    return positive.with_name(f"{positive.stem}.benchmark-manifest.json")

   782	def main() -> None:
   783	    args = parse_args()
   784	    if args.batch_size <= 0 or args.count_per_role % args.batch_size:
   785	        raise SystemExit("--batch-size must be positive and divide --count-per-role")
   786	    if args.call_timeout_seconds <= 0:
   787	        raise SystemExit("--call-timeout-seconds must be positive")
   788	    source_paths = [*args.audit_corpus, *args.prior_batch]
   789	    if not source_paths:
   790	        raise SystemExit("supply at least one --audit-corpus or --prior-batch")
   791	    if len({str(Path(path).resolve()) for path in source_paths}) != len(source_paths):
   792	        raise SystemExit("duplicate audit/prior source path")
   793	
   794	    audit_inputs, inventories, audit_families = load_audit_sources(source_paths)
   795	    positive_path = Path(args.positive_output).resolve()
   796	    restraint_path = Path(args.restraint_output).resolve()
   797	    started = time.time()
   798	    prompt_records: list[dict[str, Any]] = []
   799	    failures: list[dict[str, str]] = []
   800	
   801	    if args.mode == "generate":
   802	        positive_specs = balanced_specs("positive_list", args.count_per_role, args.seed)
   803	        restraint_specs = balanced_specs("prose_restraint", args.count_per_role, args.seed + 1)
   804	        positive_rows, records, failed, generated_inputs, generated_families = generate_role(
   805	            "positive_list", positive_specs, args, audit_inputs, audit_families
   806	        )
   807	        prompt_records.extend(records)
   808	        failures.extend(failed)
   809	        restraint_rows, records, failed, _, _ = generate_role(
   810	            "prose_restraint", restraint_specs, args, generated_inputs, generated_families
   811	        )
   812	        prompt_records.extend(records)
   813	        failures.extend(failed)
   814	        ensure_source_snapshots_unchanged(inventories)
   815	        write_jsonl(positive_path, positive_rows)
   816	        write_jsonl(restraint_path, restraint_rows)
   817	    elif not positive_path.is_file() or not restraint_path.is_file():
   818	        raise SystemExit("--mode validate requires existing positive and restraint outputs")
   819	
   820	    positive_rows, positive_report = validate_complete_corpus(
   821	        positive_path, "positive_list", args.count_per_role, audit_inputs, audit_families
   822	    )
   823	    positive_as_audit = [
   824	        as_audit_input(str(positive_path), row["id"], row["input"]) for row in positive_rows
   825	    ]
   826	    positive_families = audit_families | {normalized(row["family"]) for row in positive_rows}
   827	    _, restraint_report = validate_complete_corpus(
   828	        restraint_path,
   829	        "prose_restraint",
   830	        args.count_per_role,
   831	        [*audit_inputs, *positive_as_audit],
   832	        positive_families,
   833	    )
   834	    ensure_source_snapshots_unchanged(inventories)
   835	
   836	    generator_path = Path(__file__).resolve()
   837	    generator_sha = file_sha256(generator_path)
   838	    manifest = {
   839	        "status": "candidate_requires_independent_native_review",
   840	        "model_blind": True,
   841	        "frozen": False,
   842	        "training_eligible": False,
   843	        "native_reviewed": False,
   844	        "created_at_epoch": started,
   845	        "elapsed_seconds": round(time.time() - started, 3),
   846	        "mode": args.mode,
   847	        "generator": str(generator_path),
   848	        "generator_sha256": generator_sha,
   849	        "model": args.model if args.mode == "generate" else None,
   850	        "batch_id": args.batch_id,
   851	        "seed": args.seed,
   852	        "count_per_role": args.count_per_role,
   853	        "batch_size": args.batch_size,
   854	        "call_timeout_seconds": args.call_timeout_seconds,
   855	        "similarity_thresholds": SIMILARITY_THRESHOLDS,
   856	        "audit_sources": inventories,
   857	        "audit_source_roles": {
   858	            "training_and_eval": [str(Path(path).resolve()) for path in args.audit_corpus],
   859	            "prior_generated_batches": [str(Path(path).resolve()) for path in args.prior_batch],
   860	        },
   861	        "prompt_batches": prompt_records,
   862	        "rejected_attempts": failures,
   863	        "outputs": {
   864	            "positive_list": positive_report,
   865	            "prose_restraint": restraint_report,
   866	        },
   867	    }
   868	    output_manifest = manifest_path(args)
   869	
   870	    def verify_before_manifest_link() -> None:
   871	        ensure_source_snapshots_unchanged(inventories)
   872	        for report, label in (
   873	            (positive_report, "positive corpus"),
   874	            (restraint_report, "restraint corpus"),
   875	        ):
   876	            if file_sha256(Path(report["path"])) != report["sha256"]:
   877	                raise RuntimeError(f"{label} changed before manifest publication")
   878	        if file_sha256(generator_path) != generator_sha:
   879	            raise RuntimeError("generator changed before manifest publication")
   880	
   881	    publish_bytes_exclusive(
   882	        output_manifest,
   883	        (
   884	            json.dumps(manifest, ensure_ascii=False, indent=2, sort_keys=True) + "\n"
   885	        ).encode("utf-8"),
   886	        before_link=verify_before_manifest_link,
   887	    )
   888	    print(json.dumps({
   889	        "manifest": str(output_manifest),
   890	        "manifest_sha256": file_sha256(output_manifest),
    70	DEFAULT_CONTRACT = REPO_ROOT / "scripts/eval/contracts/eg1_multilingual_development_leakage_scanner_v1.json"

   109	class EmbeddingBackend(Protocol):
   110	    runtime_versions: dict[str, str]
   220	        path = Path(raw_path).expanduser().resolve()
   221	        if not path.is_file() or path.is_symlink():

   226	
   227	def extract_texts(rows: Sequence[dict[str, Any]]) -> list[str]:
   228	    fields = ("asr_input", "gold_output", "input", "output", "expected_output")
   229	    return [value for row in rows for field in fields if isinstance((value := row.get(field)), str) and value.strip()]
   230	
   231	
   232	def text_sides(rows: Sequence[dict[str, Any]], role: str) -> tuple[list[str], list[str]]:
   233	    if role == "benchmark":
   234	        input_fields, output_fields = ("asr_input",), ("gold_output",)
   235	    elif role in RAW_ROLES:
   236	        input_fields, output_fields = ("asr_input", "input"), ("expected_output", "gold_output", "output")
   237	    else:
   238	        return [], []
   239	    inputs = [value for row in rows for field in input_fields if isinstance((value := row.get(field)), str) and value.strip()]
   240	    outputs = [value for row in rows for field in output_fields if isinstance((value := row.get(field)), str) and value.strip()]
   241	    return inputs, outputs
   242	
   243	
   244	def token_ngrams(value: str, width: int) -> set[tuple[str, ...]]:
   245	    tokens = v2.normalize_text(value).split()
   246	    return {tuple(tokens[index:index + width]) for index in range(max(0, len(tokens) - width + 1))}
   247	
   248	
   249	def character_ngrams(value: str, width: int) -> set[str]:
   250	    normalized = v2.normalize_text(value)
   251	    return {normalized[index:index + width] for index in range(max(0, len(normalized) - width + 1))}
   252	
   253	
   254	def jaccard(left: set[Any], right: set[Any]) -> float:
   255	    if not left and not right:
   256	        return 0.0
   257	    if not left or not right:
   258	        return 0.0
   259	    return len(left & right) / len(left | right)
   260	
   261	
   262	def pairwise_jaccard_stats(candidates: Sequence[str], references: Sequence[str], *, width: int, characters: bool, threshold: float | None) -> tuple[float, int | None]:
   263	    builder = character_ngrams if characters else token_ngrams
   264	    candidate_sets = [builder(value, width) for value in candidates]
   265	    reference_sets = [builder(value, width) for value in references]
   266	    maximum = 0.0
   267	    violations = 0
   268	    for left in candidate_sets:
   269	        for right in reference_sets:
   270	            score = jaccard(left, right)
   271	            maximum = max(maximum, score)
   272	            if threshold is not None and score >= threshold:
   273	                violations += 1
   274	    return maximum, violations if threshold is not None else None
   275	
   276	
   277	def cosine_stats(candidates: Sequence[str], references: Sequence[str], backend: EmbeddingBackend, threshold: float | None) -> tuple[float, int | None]:
   278	    if not candidates or not references:
   279	        return 0.0, 0
   280	    preflight = getattr(backend, "preflight_token_lengths", None)
   281	    if callable(preflight):
   282	        preflight(candidates)
   283	        preflight(references)
   284	    left = backend.encode_documents(candidates)
   285	    right = backend.encode_documents(references)
   286	    scores = np.clip(left @ right.T, -1.0, 1.0)
   287	    return float(np.max(scores)), (int(np.count_nonzero(scores >= threshold)) if threshold is not None else None)
   288

   780	            raise ValueError(f"source receipt binding is stale: {key}")
   781	        if entry.get("producer_receipt_sha256") != sha256_bytes(get(receipt_path)):
   782	            raise ValueError(f"source inventory receipt hash is stale: {key}")

   785	def scan(*, contract: dict[str, Any], benchmark_rows: Sequence[dict[str, Any]], sources: dict[tuple[str, str], Path], backend_name: str, embedding_backend: EmbeddingBackend, source_bytes: dict[tuple[str, str], bytes] | None = None) -> tuple[list[dict[str, Any]], str]:
   786	    candidate_inputs, candidate_outputs = text_sides(benchmark_rows, "benchmark")
   787	    candidate_texts = candidate_inputs + candidate_outputs
   788	    if not candidate_texts:
   789	        raise ValueError("benchmark has no scannable text")
   790	    results = []
   791	    synthetic_thresholds = contract["synthetic_test_only_thresholds"]
   792	    for (role, name), path in sorted(sources.items()):
   793	        value = source_bytes[(role, name)] if source_bytes is not None else path.read_bytes()
   794	        rows = read_records_bytes(value, f"{role}:{name}")
   795	        texts = extract_texts(rows)
   796	        source = {"role": role, "name": name, "sha256": sha256_bytes(value), "record_count": len(rows), "authenticated_raw_text_count": len(texts)}
   797	        methods: dict[str, Any] = {}
   798	        if role in REGISTRY_ROLES:
   799	            if texts:
   800	                raise ValueError(f"{role}:{name} registry unexpectedly contains raw text")
   801	            registry_values = {str(value) for row in rows for value in row.values() if isinstance(value, str)}
   802	            if role == "blocked_text_hash_registry":
   803	                candidate_values = {
   804	                    sha256_bytes(v2.normalize_text(value).encode("utf-8"))
   805	                    for value in candidate_texts
   806	                }
   807	            else:
   808	                candidate_values = {
   809	                    str(row["semantic_family_id"])
   810	                    for row in benchmark_rows
   811	                    if isinstance(row.get("semantic_family_id"), str)
   812	                }
   813	            matches = candidate_values & registry_values
   814	            methods["exact_normalized"] = {"status": "passed" if not matches else "failed", "matches": len(matches)}
   815	            for method in METHODS[1:]:
   816	                methods[method] = {"status": "not_applicable_no_raw_text", "authenticated_raw_text_count": 0}
   817	        elif role in RAW_ROLES:
   818	            if not texts:
   819	                raise ValueError(f"{role}:{name} has no authenticated raw text")
   820	            normalized_candidates = [v2.normalize_text(value) for value in candidate_texts]
   821	            normalized_sources = [v2.normalize_text(value) for value in texts]
   822	            exact = set(normalized_candidates) & set(normalized_sources)
   823	            exact_violations = sum(left == right for left in normalized_candidates for right in normalized_sources)
   824	            thresholds = synthetic_thresholds if backend_name == "synthetic_test_only" else {method: None for method in METHODS[1:]}
   825	            source_inputs, source_outputs = text_sides(rows, role)
   826	            embedding_axes = {
   827	                "input_input": cosine_stats(candidate_inputs, source_inputs, embedding_backend, thresholds["embedding_cosine"]),
   828	                "output_output": cosine_stats(candidate_outputs, source_outputs, embedding_backend, thresholds["embedding_cosine"]),
   829	                "input_output": cosine_stats(candidate_inputs, source_outputs, embedding_backend, thresholds["embedding_cosine"]),
   830	                "output_input": cosine_stats(candidate_outputs, source_inputs, embedding_backend, thresholds["embedding_cosine"]),
   831	            }
   832	            embedding_axis_counts = {
   833	                "input_input": len(candidate_inputs) * len(source_inputs),
   834	                "output_output": len(candidate_outputs) * len(source_outputs),
   835	                "input_output": len(candidate_inputs) * len(source_outputs),
   836	                "output_input": len(candidate_outputs) * len(source_inputs),
   837	            }
   838	            scores = {
   839	                "token_ngram_jaccard": pairwise_jaccard_stats(candidate_texts, texts, width=contract["token_ngram_width"], characters=False, threshold=thresholds["token_ngram_jaccard"]),
   840	                "character_ngram_jaccard": pairwise_jaccard_stats(candidate_texts, texts, width=contract["character_ngram_width"], characters=True, threshold=thresholds["character_ngram_jaccard"]),
   841	                "embedding_cosine": (max(value[0] for value in embedding_axes.values()), None if backend_name == "production" else sum(value[1] or 0 for value in embedding_axes.values())),
   842	            }
   843	            comparisons = len(candidate_texts) * len(texts)
   844	            methods["exact_normalized"] = {"status": "passed" if not exact else "failed", "matches": len(exact), "comparison_count": comparisons, "violation_count": exact_violations}
   845	            for method, (score, violation_count) in scores.items():
   846	                if backend_name == "production":
   847	                    status, threshold = "calibration_required", None
   848	                else:
   849	                    threshold = synthetic_thresholds[method]
   850	                    status = threshold_status(score, threshold)
   851	                methods[method] = {"status": status, "maximum_score": round(score, 8), "threshold": threshold, "comparison_count": comparisons, "violation_count": violation_count}
   852	                if method == "embedding_cosine":
   853	                    methods[method]["comparison_count"] = len(candidate_inputs) * len(source_inputs) + len(candidate_outputs) * len(source_outputs) + len(candidate_inputs) * len(source_outputs) + len(candidate_outputs) * len(source_inputs)
   854	                    methods[method]["axes"] = {axis: {"maximum_score": round(values[0], 8), "violation_count": values[1], "comparison_count": embedding_axis_counts[axis]} for axis, values in embedding_axes.items()}
   855	        else:
   856	            raise ValueError(f"unsupported leakage role: {role}")
   857	        results.append({"source": source, "methods": methods})
   858	    all_exact = all(row["methods"]["exact_normalized"]["status"] == "passed" for row in results)
   859	    if backend_name == "production":
   860	        overall = "calibration_required_noncertifying" if all_exact else "failed"
   861	    else:
   862	        statuses = [method["status"] for row in results for method in row["methods"].values()]
   863	        overall = "synthetic_not_quality_evidence" if all(value in {"passed", "not_applicable_no_raw_text"} for value in statuses) else "failed"
   864	    return results, overall
   865	
   866	
   867	def build_expected_receipt(*, backend_name: str, status: str, benchmark_bytes: bytes, benchmark_rows: Sequence[dict[str, Any]], contract_bytes: bytes, inventory_bytes: bytes, blocked_receipt_bytes: bytes, controls: dict[str, str], expected_head: str, tree: dict[str, Any] | None, runtime_versions: dict[str, str], results: list[dict[str, Any]]) -> dict[str, Any]:
   868	    return {
   869	        "schema_version": RECEIPT_SCHEMA_VERSION,
   870	        "status": status,
   871	        "backend": backend_name,
   872	        "benchmark_sha256": sha256_bytes(benchmark_bytes),
   873	        "benchmark_content_sha256": v2.benchmark_content_sha256(benchmark_rows),
   874	        "contract_sha256": sha256_bytes(contract_bytes),
   875	        "source_inventory_sha256": sha256_bytes(inventory_bytes),
   876	        "blocked_registry_receipt_sha256": sha256_bytes(blocked_receipt_bytes),
   877	        "scanner_provenance": {"scanner_id": SCHEMA_VERSION, "scanner_path": str(SCRIPT_PATH.relative_to(REPO_ROOT)), "scanner_sha256": controls["scanner_sha256"], "replay_backend_path": REPLAY_BACKEND_PATH, "replay_backend_sha256": controls["replay_backend_sha256"], "replay_inventory_path": REPLAY_INVENTORY_PATH_RELATIVE, "replay_inventory_sha256": controls["replay_inventory_sha256"], "replay_normalizer_path": REPLAY_NORMALIZER_PATH_RELATIVE, "replay_normalizer_sha256": controls["replay_normalizer_sha256"], "multilingual_validator_path": V2_PATH_RELATIVE, "multilingual_validator_sha256": controls["multilingual_validator_sha256"], "producing_git_head": expected_head, "execution_status": status, "operator_attested_execution": False, "assurance_scope": "development_only_nonrelease"},
   878	        "embedding_runtime": {"model_tree": tree, "runtime_versions": runtime_versions},
   879	        "results": results,
   880	        "contains_raw_text": False,
   881	    }

   887	    reserved = False

   890	        reserved = True

   130	def model_family(config: dict[str, Any]) -> str:
   131	    model_type = str(config.get("model_type", "")).casefold().replace("_", "")
   132	    architectures = " ".join(str(item) for item in config.get("architectures", []))
   133	    if "qwen35" in model_type or "qwen3_5" in architectures.casefold():
   134	        return QWEN35_FAMILY
   135	    if "gemma" in model_type or "gemma" in architectures.casefold():
   136	        return "gemma"
   137	    return "other"
   138	
   139	
   140	def resolve_training_mode(family: str, requested: str) -> str:
   141	    resolved = BF16_LORA_MODE if family == QWEN35_FAMILY else QLORA_MODE
   142	    if requested != "auto":
   143	        resolved = requested
   144	    if family == QWEN35_FAMILY and resolved == QLORA_MODE:
   145	        raise ValueError(
   146	            "Refusing Qwen3.5 QLoRA: Unsloth recommends BF16 LoRA because its "
   147	            "4-bit quantization differences are higher than normal"
   148	        )
   149	    if family != QWEN35_FAMILY and resolved == BF16_LORA_MODE:
   150	        raise ValueError("BF16 LoRA is currently implemented only for Qwen3.5")
   151	    return resolved

  1337	        write_json(output_dir / "training-manifest.json", manifest)
  1338	
  1339	    def to_text(row: dict[str, Any]) -> dict[str, str]:
  1340	        messages = [
  1341	            {"role": "system", "content": system_prompt},
  1342	            {
  1343	                "role": "user",
  1344	                "content": f"<TRANSCRIPT>\n{row['input']}\n</TRANSCRIPT>",
  1345	            },
  1346	            {"role": "assistant", "content": row["output"]},
  1347	        ]
  1348	        return {
  1349	            "text": tokenizer.apply_chat_template(
  1350	                messages,
  1351	                tokenize=False,
  1352	                add_generation_prompt=False,
  1353	                enable_thinking=False,
  1354	            )
  1355	        }
  1356	
  1357	    rendered_rows = [to_text(row) for row in rows]
  1358	    response_marker = response_marker_for_family(family)
  1359	    response_marker_counts = [row["text"].count(response_marker) for row in rendered_rows]
  1360	    if args.preflight_only:
  1361	        try:
  1362	            response_marker, response_marker_counts = validate_response_markers(
  1363	                [row["text"] for row in rendered_rows], family

  1365	        except ValueError as error:

  1367	    manifest["response_mask_receipt"] = {
  1368	        "marker": response_marker,
  1369	        "rendered_marker_counts": response_marker_counts,
  1370	    }
  1371	    dataset = Dataset.from_list(rendered_rows).shuffle(seed=args.seed)
  1372	    trainer_kwargs: dict[str, Any] = {
  1373	        "model": model,

  1375	        "args": sft_config(
  1376	            SFTConfig,

  1382	                int(QWEN35_PREFLIGHT_CONTRACT["max_steps"])
  1383	                if args.preflight_only
  1384	                else -1
  1385	            ),

  1387	            warmup_ratio=warmup_ratio,
  1388	            lr_scheduler_type=scheduler,
  1389	            logging_steps=1 if args.preflight_only else 10,
  1390	            optim="adamw_8bit",
  1391	            weight_decay=0.01,

  1396	            seed=args.seed,

  1398	        ),
  1399	    }
  1400	    trainer_parameters = set(inspect.signature(SFTTrainer.__init__).parameters)
  1401	    if "tokenizer" in trainer_parameters:
  1402	        trainer_kwargs["tokenizer"] = tokenizer
  1403	    elif "processing_class" in trainer_parameters:
  1404	        trainer_kwargs["processing_class"] = tokenizer
  1405	    else:
  1406	        raise RuntimeError("SFTTrainer accepts neither tokenizer nor processing_class")
  1407	    trainer = SFTTrainer(**trainer_kwargs)
  1408	
  1409	    if family == "gemma":
  1410	        trainer = train_on_responses_only(
  1411	            trainer,
  1412	            instruction_part="<|turn>user\n",
  1413	            response_part="<|turn>model\n",
  1414	        )

  1417	            trainer,
  1418	            instruction_part="<|im_start|>user\n",
  1419	            response_part="<|im_start|>assistant\n",
  1420	        )
  1421	
  1422	    if args.preflight_only:
  1423	        labeled_token_counts: list[int] = []
  1424	        for sample_index in range(len(trainer.train_dataset)):
  1425	            sample = trainer.train_dataset[sample_index]
  1426	            if "labels" not in sample:
  1427	                raise RuntimeError(
  1428	                    f"Response-only loss mask produced no labels for sample {sample_index}"
  1429	                )
  1430	            labeled_tokens = sum(token != -100 for token in sample["labels"])
